We illustrate our approach in reaching a distant object with different sticks, scooping candies from a bowl using diverse items, and using different boxes or crates as stepping platforms to retrieve an object from a high shelf. Our baseline comparisons show that identifying causal features and grounding them in physical tool properties leads to more reliable tool selection and stronger skill keypoint transfer.

</details>


### [72] [Contact-Free Grasp Stability Prediction with In-Hand Time-of-Flight Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05461)
*Kyle DuFrene,Cindy Grimm*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多区域飞行时间传感器的非接触式抓取稳定性预测方法，无需抓取物体即可预测，在15Hz频率下实现85.5%-86.0%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取规划方法在噪声传感器等因素下性能下降，而基于触觉的抓取稳定性分类器需要接触并抓取物体才能检测失败，效率低下。

Method: 在夹爪远端连杆安装多区域飞行时间传感器，收集2500+次真实抓取数据训练分类器，在15Hz频率下进行非接触式稳定性预测。

Result: 验证集准确率85.5%，测试集（3个未见物体）准确率86.0%。

Conclusion: 非接触式抓取稳定性预测方法显著加快了稳定性分类过程，在无需实际抓取物体的情况下即可有效预测抓取稳定性。

Abstract: Current approaches to grasp planning for robotics demonstrate high success rates, but degrade with noisy sensors and other factors. Previous works have proposed tactile-based grasp stability classifiers to detect failures, but these approaches rely on making contact and grasping the object to do so. We propose a contact-free grasp stability predictor using multi-zone time-of-flight sensors mounted in the distal links of a gripper. Our method, as it does not require grasping the object to make a prediction, significantly speeds up the stability classification process, cycling at 15 Hz. We collected over 2,500 real-world grasps across 15 objects to train a classifier. Additionally, we conducted grasp attempts over six additional unseen objects, three for validation and model selection, and three for model testing. Our approach demonstrated strong classification performance, with an accuracy of 85.5% on validation and 86.0% on test objects.

</details>


### [73] [Real-world Latency Analysis of Vehicular Visible Light Communication with Multiple LED Transmitters and an Event-Based Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05541)
*Ryota Soga,Tsukasa Shimizu,Shintaro Shiba,Quan Kong,Shan Lu,Takaya Yamazato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于事件相机的可见光通信（VLC）系统，解决了带宽饱和、多发射机接收和延迟表征三大挑战，采用正事件模式及协议设计抑制事件生成、实现多LED同时接收，并在真实车辆场景中验证了端到端延迟满足协同感知需求，证明事件相机VLC可作为现有V2X技术的可行补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有高时间分辨率、低延迟和宽动态范围等优势，是可见光通信（VLC）在车联万物（V2X）应用中的有前景的接收器。然而现有系统面临带宽饱和、多发射机接收以及延迟表征等关键挑战，亟需解决。

Method: 采用正事件模式，设计协议抑制事件生成同时保持通信距离和大视场；提出多发射机识别方法，实现同时接收最多三个LED信号；在真实车辆场景中评估端到端延迟。

Result: 实现了多发射机同时接收（最多三个LED），并在真实车辆场景中验证了端到端延迟满足协同感知需求。

Conclusion: 基于事件相机的VLC可作为现有V2X技术（如RF）的可行补充方案。

Abstract: Event cameras offer high temporal resolution, low latency, and wide dynamic range, making them promising receivers for visible light communication (VLC) in vehicle-to-everything (V2X) applications. This work presents an event-camera-based VLC system addressing three key challenges: bandwidth saturation, multi-transmitter reception, and latency characterization.
  We adopt a positive-event-only mode and design a protocol that suppresses event generation while maintaining communication distance and a wide field of view. We also propose a method to identify multiple transmitters and demonstrate simultaneous reception from up to three LEDs. Finally, we evaluate end-to-end latency in real vehicular scenarios and show that the system meets cooperative perception requirements. These results demonstrate that event-camera-based VLC is a feasible complement to existing V2X technologies (e.g., RF).

</details>


### [74] [On the Emergence of Pendular Structure in Multi-Contact Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05707)
*Lingxue Lyu,Zihui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了线倒立摆模型（LIPM）在腿足运动控制中并非作为控制器成本偏好，而是作为建模选择的普遍现象。通过一个惩罚角动量变化率的小型质心最优控制问题，分析了其最优解的三个特征：满秩支撑下最优解趋向钟摆力模式（由力矩雅可比矩阵的SVD决定，足部几何常数与实验误差在16%以内）；双足支撑（如小跑）时摩擦锥引入$\|\dot{H}_G\|$的下界且无法通过权重调节消除，并存在临界水平加速度处的非平滑可行性拐点；添加非零$\dot{H}_G$任务项会使最优解以可预测方式偏离钟摆集合。这些结果与经典ZMP/DCM理论接近，并在点质量四足机器人和Unitree Go1上通过MuJoCo仿真（开环QP和闭环力矩控制）验证，同时指出了渐近分析在闭环控制中失效的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有腿足运动控制广泛采用LIPM但仅作为建模假设，其与控制器实际优化目标之间的理论联系缺乏显式分析。本文旨在揭示惩罚角动量变化率的最优控制问题与LIPM行为之间的内在数学关联，并量化不同支撑条件下的偏差来源（如摩擦锥约束、任务项引入的非零角动量变化率）。

Method: 基于小型质心最优控制问题（OCP），以角动量变化率$\|\dot{H}_G\|$为惩罚项，结合力矩雅可比矩阵的奇异值分解（SVD）推导最优力模式。针对双足支撑（N=2）引入摩擦锥约束，分析可行性拐点与临界水平加速度的闭式解。通过点质量四足模型和Unitree Go1在MuJoCo中进行开环QP与闭环力矩控制仿真验证。

Result: 满秩支撑下最优力模式趋向钟摆模式，频率由力矩雅可比矩阵的SVD决定，足部几何常数预测与实验误差≤16%；双足支撑时摩擦锥强制$\|\dot{H}_G\|$存在非零下界，且存在水平加速度临界值处的可行性非平滑转折；引入非零$\dot{H}_G$任务项后最优解可预测地偏离钟摆集；仿真验证了开环QP下的渐近行为，但闭环控制中该描述存在适用边界。

Conclusion: LIPM在最优控制框架下可被解释为惩罚角动量变化率的自然结果，但双足支撑等场景因摩擦锥约束导致固有偏差。该发现统一了LIPM与ZMP/DCM理论，并指出了在实际闭环控制中渐近分析的限制条件，为腿足机器人控制器的设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: LIPM is everywhere in legged-locomotion control, but almost always as a modeling choice rather than as something the
  controller's cost actually prefers. This note tries to make that link more explicit. Working from a small centroidal
  OCP that penalizes the rate of angular momentum, we look at what its optimum tends to look like. Three things come
  out. With full-rank stance, the optimum drifts toward a pendular force pattern at a rate determined by the SVD of the
  moment Jacobian; the constant is set by foot-span geometry and matches the experiments to within 16%. With N=2 stance,
  as in trot, the friction cone introduces a lower bound on $\|\dot{H}_G\|$ that no amount of weight tuning fixes; we
  also see a non-smooth feasibility kink at a critical horizontal acceleration that we can write in closed form. Adding
  a task term that asks for a nonzero $\dot{H}_G$ moves the optimum off the pendular set in a predictable way. None of
  this is far from the classical ZMP/DCM picture. We test these claims on a point-mass quadruped and on the Unitree Go1
  in MuJoCo (open-loop QP and a torque-level closed-loop controller), and we note where the asymptotic story stops being
  a good description of what the closed loop actually does.

</details>


### [75] [MaMi-HOI: Harmonizing Global Kinematics and Local Geometry for Human-Object Interaction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05756)
*Hao Wang,Shiqi Wang,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MaMi-HOI框架，通过几何感知邻近适配器（GAPA）和运动和谐适配器（KHA）解决扩散模型中的"几何遗忘"问题，在保持自然运动的同时实现精确的人-物交互接触，并支持长期复杂轨迹任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在语义对齐上表现优异，但难以维持精确的物体接触。作者发现一个关键现象"几何遗忘"：随着扩散模型深度增加，语义特征会掩盖物体几何特征，导致模型丧失对物体几何的感知能力。

Method: 提出MaMi-HOI层级框架：1）几何感知邻近适配器（GAPA），显式重新注入密集物体细节，进行残差修正以实现精确接触；2）运动和谐适配器（KHA），主动对齐全身姿态与空间目标，确保骨骼在满足约束的同时保持自然性。

Result: 实验验证MaMi-HOI同时实现了自然运动与精确接触，并将生成能力扩展到具有复杂轨迹的长期任务，弥合了全局导航与高保真操作之间的差距。

Conclusion: MaMi-HOI通过宏观运动流畅性与微观空间精度的协调，有效解决了几何遗忘问题，在3D人-物交互生成任务中达到最优性能。代码已开源。

Abstract: Generating realistic 3D Human-Object Interactions (HOI) is a fundamental task for applications ranging from embodied AI to virtual content creation, which requires harmonizing high-level semantic intent with strict low-level physical constraints. Existing methods excel at semantic alignment, however, they struggle to maintain precise object contact. We reveal a key finding termed \textit{Geometric Forgetting}: as diffusion model depth increases, semantic feature tend to overshadow object geometry feature, causing the model to lose its perception to object geometry. To address this, we propose MaMi-HOI, a hierarchical framework reconciling \textbf{Ma}cro-level kinematic fluidity with \textbf{Mi}cro-level spatial precision. First, to counteract geometric forgetting, we introduce the Geometry-Aware Proximity Adapter (GAPA), which explicitly re-injects dense object details to perform residual snapping corrections for precise contact. Nevertheless, such aggressive local enforcement can disrupt global dynamics, leading to robotic stiffness. In response, we introduce the Kinematic Harmony Adapter (KHA), which proactively aligns whole-body posture with spatial objectives, ensuring the skeleton actively accommodates constraints without compromising naturalness. Extensive experiments validate that MaMi-HOI simultaneously achieves natural motion and precise contact. Crucially, it extends generation capabilities to long-term tasks with complex trajectories, effectively bridging the gap between global navigation and high-fidelity manipulation in 3D scenes. Code is available at https://github.com/DON738110198/MaMi-HOI.git

</details>


### [76] [A Comparative Study of INDI and NDI with Nonlinear Disturbance Observer for Aerial Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05825)
*Benedetta Rota,Mirko Mizzoni,Amr Afifi,Pieter van Goor,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过仿真对比分析了增量非线性动态逆（INDI）与非线性动态逆+非线性扰动观测器（NDI+NDO）在全驱动飞行机器人中的鲁棒性，结果表明INDI在模型失配和组合应力场景下鲁棒性更强，而NDI+NDO虽在标称性能上相当，但在非理想条件下更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较INDI和NDI+NDO两种先进非线性控制策略在全驱动飞行机器人中的鲁棒性优劣，为实际应用提供选型指导。

Method: 采用基于仿真的对比分析方法，在代表性运行场景下系统性地评估两种方法在参数变化、外部扰动和测量噪声下的跟踪性能、鲁棒性和控制能量消耗。

Result: INDI在多种模型失配和组合应力场景下表现出更强的鲁棒性；NDI+NDO在标称条件下性能与INDI相当，但在多种非理想条件下更为敏感。

Conclusion: 研究为飞行机器人应用中增量式与基于观测器的逆控制策略的相对优势和局限性提供了实践指导。

Abstract: This work presents a simulation-based comparative robustness analysis of Incremental Nonlinear Dynamic Inversion (INDI) and Nonlinear Dynamic Inversion augmented with a nonlinear disturbance observer (NDI+NDO) for fully actuated aerial robots. A systematic simulation campaign across representative operating scenarios is conducted, where we compare tracking performance, robustness, control effort, under parametric variations, external disturbances, and measurement noise. Results show that INDI demonstrates stronger robustness in several model-mismatch and combined-stress cases, while NDI+NDO primarily matches nominal performance but exhibits greater sensitivity under several non-ideal conditions. These findings provide practical guidance on the relative strengths and limitations of incremental and observer-based inversion strategies for aerial robotic applications.

</details>


### [77] [Cycle-resolved Cephalopod-Inspired Pulsed-Jet Robot With High-Volume Expulsion and Drag-Reduced Gliding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05875)
*Yiyuan Zhang,Anye Zhong,Junkai Chen,Wenci Xin,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受头足类动物脉冲式喷射推进启发，本文提出了一种刚柔混合折纸外套膜结构的脉冲喷射机器人，实现75%有效腔体体积缩减和75.7%横截面阻力面积降低，峰值速度达0.5 m/s（3.8 BL/s），并建立了喷射-滑行-填充的周期解析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头足类动物的脉冲式喷射推进并非单一喷射事件，而是包含喷射、被动滑行和外套膜再填充的协调循环过程。现有机器人往往只模拟孤立喷射，缺乏对完整循环动力学的研究，因此需要构建能够实现周期解析的仿生平台。

Method: 设计了刚柔混合折纸外套膜：刚性折叠面板与柔性硅胶框架结合，实现大变形几何引导的主动驱动。通过该平台建立了周期解析框架，分别研究喷射体积、滑行时长和填充路径对整体循环推进性能的影响。

Result: 机器人峰值速度约0.5 m/s（3.8 BL/s），首个喷射周期内平均速度超过0.2 m/s（1.5 BL/s）。高喷射体积比收缩对速度生成起关键作用，减阻滑行形态在不同滑行时长下均有效，外套膜孔启发的被动进气阀辅助填充。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅提供了主动变形头足类喷射推进的机器人实现，还建立了研究脉冲喷射中喷射-滑行-填充动力学的统一实验平台，揭示了周期中各阶段对整体推进性能的贡献机制。

Abstract: Cephalopod pulsed-jet locomotion is not a single isolated expulsion event, but a coordinated cycle involving jet expulsion, passive gliding, and mantle refilling. Inspired by this cycle-resolved biological strategy, this paper presents a cephalopod-inspired pulsed-jet robot with a rigid-soft hybrid origami mantle that enables large, actively driven, and geometry-guided body deformation. The proposed mantle integrates rigid folding panels with a compliant silicone framework, allowing a 75% effective cavity-volume reduction during expulsion and reducing the projected cross-sectional drag area by approximately 75.7% in the contracted gliding configuration. Using this platform, we formulate a cycle-resolved framework to separately investigate how expelled volume, glide duration, and refill pathway influence whole-cycle locomotion performance. Experiments show that the robot reaches a peak speed of approximately 0.5 m/s (3.8 BL/s) and an average speed exceeding 0.2 m/s (1.5 BL/s) within the first jetting cycle. The results further demonstrate the roles of high expelled-volume-ratio contraction in speed generation, reduced-drag-area gliding under different glide durations, and mantle-aperture-inspired passive inlet valves in assisting refill. This work provides both a robotic implementation of actively deformable cephalopod-like jet propulsion and a unified experimental platform for studying expulsion-gliding-refilling dynamics in pulsed-jet locomotion.

</details>


### [78] [Generating Roadside LiDAR Datasets from Vehicle-Side Datasets via Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05897)
*Yuhan Xia,Runxin Zhao,Hanyang Zhuang,Chunxiang Wang,Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VRS框架，通过车辆点云补全和占用可见性约束，将车载LiDAR数据合成为路侧LiDAR数据，有效缓解路侧标注数据稀缺问题，提升路侧3D检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路侧感知虽能扩展感知覆盖、提升交通安全，但大规模标注的路侧LiDAR数据集稀缺，导致难以训练高性能路侧感知模型。

Method: 提出VRS框架：(1) 利用车辆点云补全补偿缺失几何信息；(2) 引入基于占用度的可见性约束处理跨视角渲染中的大视角变化；(3) 实现灵活的多视角渲染，实现可扩展的路侧数据生成。

Result: 在路侧3D目标检测上的实验表明，合成数据有效补充了真实路侧数据，缓解了真实路侧数据有限的局限性，并提升了对未见路侧视角的泛化能力。

Conclusion: VRS框架能够从车载数据生成高质量的路侧LiDAR合成数据，为解决路侧感知中数据稀缺问题提供有效方案。

Abstract: Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS) require reliable environmental perception to support safe and efficient transportation. With the rapid development of Vehicle-to-everything (V2X), roadside perception has become an effective means to extend sensing coverage and improve traffic safety. However, the scarcity of large-scale annotated roadside LiDAR datasets poses a major challenge for training high-performance roadside perception models. In this paper, we introduce Vehicle-to-Roadside LiDAR Synthesis (VRS), a data synthesis framework that generates labeled roadside LiDAR datasets from vehicle-side datasets via LiDAR novel view synthesis. To mitigate the vehicle-to-roadside domain gap, VRS employs vehicle point cloud completion to compensate for missing geometry in vehicle-side observations, and introduces an occupancy-based visibility constraint to handle large viewpoint changes during cross-view rendering. The proposed framework enables flexible multi-view rendering for scalable roadside data generation. Extensive experiments on roadside 3D object detection demonstrate that the synthesized data effectively complements real roadside data, mitigates the limitations of limited real-world roadside data, and improves generalization to unseen roadside viewpoints.

</details>


### [79] [DexSynRefine: Synthesizing and Refining Human-Object Interaction Motion for Physically Feasible Dexterous Robot Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05925)
*Hyesung Lee,Hyunwoo Jung,Si-Hwan Heo,Sungwook Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexSynRefine是一个结合运动基元合成、任务空间残差强化学习和接触动力学自适应的框架，从稀疏的人-物交互演示中学习灵巧操作，在五个任务上相比运动学重定向提升50-70个百分点，并成功迁移到真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人-物交互数据中学习灵巧操作是一种可扩展的方案，但现有演示数据稀疏且仅提供运动学信息，在形态不匹配和接触丰富的动力学场景下无法直接执行。

Method: 提出三组件框架：1) HOI-MMFP，基于运动流形基元生成协调的手-物轨迹；2) 任务空间残差RL策略，在继承运动学结构的同时物理落地参考轨迹；3) 接触与动力学自适应模块，基于本体感知历史实现sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 在五个灵巧操作任务（抓取放置、工具使用、物体重定向）上，任务空间残差策略优于先前的动作表征基线，在仿真中表现优异，并成功迁移到真实机器人上，相比运动学重定向提升50-70个百分点。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架有效结合了运动基元合成与残差强化学习，解决了从稀疏HOI演示学习灵巧操作的关键挑战，并在仿真和真实场景中验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Learning dexterous manipulation from human-object interaction (HOI) data is a scalable alternative to teleoperation, but HOI demonstrations are sparse and provide only kinematic motion that is not directly executable under embodiment mismatch and contact-rich dynamics. We present DexSynRefine, a framework with three coupled components: HOI-MMFP, a task- and object-initial-state-conditioned extension of motion manifold primitives that synthesizes coordinated hand-object trajectories from sparse HOI demonstrations; a task-space residual RL policy that physically grounds the synthesized reference while inheriting its kinematic structure; and a contact-and-dynamics adaptation module that enables sim-to-real transfer from proprioceptive history. Across five dexterous manipulation tasks spanning pick-and-place, tool use, and object reorientation, our task-space residual policy outperforms prior action-representation baselines in simulations and transfers to a real robot on all five tasks, improving over kinematic retargeting by 50-70 percentage points.

</details>


### [80] [Plug-and-Play Label Map Diffusion for Universal Goal-Oriented Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05960)
*Zhixuan Shen,Yijie Zeng,Shengxiang Luo,Tianrui Li,Haonan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PLMD，一种基于扩散模型的标签地图补全方法，用于解决具身视觉中目标导向导航（GON）的挑战。PLMD通过扩散过程生成未知区域的障碍物和语义标签，无需完全地图即可定位目标，并利用障碍物先验缓解语义关联不一致问题，在三个GON任务上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于地图的GON方法依赖自我中心语义地图，面临两个主要挑战：需要完整地图来定位目标，以及语义关联不一致。PLMD旨在解决这些问题，使得机器人能够在部分观测环境中准确定位目标。

Method: PLMD定义了一种基于DDPM的扩散补全模型，通过扩散过程为未知区域生成障碍物标签和语义标签。该方法利用已知与未知区域之间障碍物布局的结构一致性，将障碍物先验集成到语义去噪过程中，从而缓解语义关联不一致问题。

Result: 实验表明PLMD：（I）有效扩展了未知地图区域；（II）可无缝集成到依赖语义地图的现有导航策略中；（III）在三个GON任务上达到了最优性能。

Conclusion: PLMD作为一种即插即用的标签地图扩散方法，通过扩散补全机制有效解决了部分观测环境下的目标定位问题，且具有良好的通用性和集成性，为GON任务提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: In embodied vision, Goal-Oriented Navigation (GON) requires robots to locate a specific goal within an unexplored environment. The primary challenge of GON arises from the need to construct a Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) map to understand the environment while simultaneously localizing an unobserved goal. Existing map-based methods typically employ self-centered semantic maps, often facing challenges such as reliance on complete maps or inconsistent semantic association. To this end, we propose Plug-and-Play Label Map Diffusion (PLMD), which defines a novel map completion diffusion model based on Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models (DDPM). PLMD generates obstacle and semantic labels for unobserved regions through a diffusion-based completion process, thereby enabling goal localization even in partially observed environments. Moreover, it mitigates inconsistent semantic association by leveraging structural consistency between known and unknown obstacle layouts and integrating obstacle priors into the semantic denoising process. By substituting predicted labels for unobserved regions, robots can accurately localize the specified objects. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PLMD \textbf{(I)} effectively expands the region of unknown maps, \textbf{(II)} integrates seamlessly into existing navigation strategies that rely on semantic maps, \textbf{(III)} achieves state-of-the-art performance on three GON tasks.

</details>


### [81] [Accurate Trajectory Tracking with MPCC for Flapping-Wing MAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06042)
*Charbel Toumieh,Jack Zeng,Niel Mistry,Dario Floreano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对鸟尺度扑翼飞行器控制难题，提出一种基于模型预测等周控制（MPCC）的轨迹跟踪方法，通过弧长参数化轨迹和在线优化进度，结合紧凑可微的耦合动力学模型，在XFly扑翼飞行器上实现了6.5-9cm的平均轨迹跟踪误差，比此前方法提升了近10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扑翼飞行器控制方法中，级联控制器独立处理高度、速度和航向，在复杂机动中产生持续跟踪误差；而时间参数化轨迹跟踪需要预定义速度剖面，现有方法无法鲁棒地处理这些耦合动力学。本文旨在同时解决这两个局限性。

Method: 提出模型预测等周控制（MPCC）方法，跟踪弧长参数化轨迹并在线优化进度，无需预定义时序；同时设计了一个紧凑、连续可微的动力学模型，捕捉鸟尺度扑翼飞行器的主要耦合效应，满足实时非线性优化的计算预算。

Result: 在XFly扑翼飞行器上沿圆形和三维竞速轨迹飞行，平均偏离参考轨迹6.5-9cm，速度达3m/s，较此前方法提升近10倍。

Conclusion: 所提MPCC方法与紧凑动力学模型有效解决了扑翼飞行器的耦合动力学控制难题，实现了高精度轨迹跟踪，验证了方法的实用性和优越性。

Abstract: Flapping-wing micro aerial vehicles offer quieter and safer operation than rotary-wing drones, yet achieving precise autonomous control of bird-scale ornithopters remains challenging: lift, airspeed, and turning authority are tightly coupled and governed by only a few control inputs. Conventional cascaded controllers treat altitude, speed, and heading independently, producing persistent tracking errors during complex maneuvers, while time-parameterized trajectory tracking requires predefined speed profiles that existing methods cannot robustly produce for these coupled dynamics. We address both limitations simultaneously with a Model Predictive Contouring Control (MPCC) approach that tracks arc-length-parameterized trajectories while optimizing progress online, eliminating the need for predefined timing. However, MPCC requires a dynamical model that captures the coupled aerodynamics without exceeding the computational budget of real-time nonlinear optimization. Here, we propose a compact, continuously differentiable model that captures the dominant couplings of bird-scale ornithopters, enabling real-time predictive control. We validated the method with the XFly ornithopter flying along circular and three-dimensional racing trajectories and achieved a mean deviation from the reference trajectory between 6.5 and 9 cm at speeds up to 3 m/s, which represents an almost 10-fold improvement over prior ornithopter control methods.

</details>


### [82] [Monitoring autonomous persistent surveillance missions using invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06062)
*Vladislav Nenchev,Prodromos Sotiriadis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了自主机器人在黑盒自主栈下进行持久监视的运行时监控问题。通过将环境分区并建模为状态依赖混合系统，提出了一种基于离线计算不变量的监控器。针对大规模空间难以获取全局不变量的问题，设计了组合式监控器，通过分布式计算各区域低维不变量并在线检查其合取。在独立性假设下，该组合监控器相对于全系统不变量是完备且可靠的。在真实机器人的迷宫中进行了案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决黑盒自主栈下持久监视的运行时监控问题，特别是当监视空间较大时，难以离线计算全局不变量的问题。

Method: 将环境分区并建模为线性参数变化的状态依赖混合系统；离线计算不变量；提出组合式监控器，通过分布式计算各区域低维不变量并在线合取检查。

Result: 在独立性假设下，组合监控器相对于全系统不变量是完备且可靠的；在真实机器人持久监视迷宫的案例中验证了实用性。

Conclusion: 组合式监控方法可有效解决黑盒自主栈下大规模空间的持久监视运行时监控问题，具有良好的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: This paper studies runtime monitoring for persistent surveillance by autonomous robots when the autonomy stack is a black box. The environment is partitioned into finitely many parts, each carrying an uncertainty state that decreases when observed and increases otherwise. We model the closed loop as a state-dependent hybrid system with linear parameter varying dynamics and design a monitor based on an invariant computed offline. As this invariant is typically hard to obtain for large to-be-surveyed spaces, we propose a compositional monitor obtained by decentralized computation of low-dimensional invariant sets for each uncertainty region, and checking their conjunction online. Under common independence assumptions, the compositional monitor is sound and complete with respect to the full-system invariant. The approach is applied in a case study with a real robot persistently monitoring a labyrinth, emphasizing its applicability in practice.

</details>


### [83] [When to Trust Imagination: Adaptive Action Execution for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06222)
*Rui Wang,Yue Zhang,Jiehong Lin,Kuncheng Luo,Jianan Wang,Zhongrui Wang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FFDC，一种轻量级验证器，用于自适应调整世界动作模型（WAM）的执行步长，通过预测-观测一致性判断是否继续执行预测的动作序列，在保持长程效率的同时提升对动态环境的响应能力。实验表明该方法在RoboTwin和真实场景中均取得了鲁棒性与效率的更好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型在执行推理后会固定执行预测动作，机器人无法感知预测的未来与实际物理过程是否一致，导致在接触密集或困难阶段缺乏灵活性。

Method: 提出未来前向动力学因果注意力（FFDC）轻量验证器，联合推理预测动作、预测视觉动态、真实观测和语言指令，估计剩余动作序列的可信度；引入混合视野训练（Mixture-of-Horizon Training）提升轨迹覆盖。

Result: 在RoboTwin基准上，WAM前向传播减少69.10%，执行时间减少34.02%，成功率提升2.54%；真实实验中成功率提升35%。

Conclusion: FFDC通过预测-观测一致性实现自适应动作块大小的涌现，在保持长程执行效率的同时恢复困难阶段的响应能力，实现了鲁棒性与效率的强权衡。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation by jointly predicting future visual observations and future actions. However, current WAMs typically execute a fixed number of predicted actions after each model inference, leaving the robot blind to whether the imagined future remains consistent with the actual physical rollout. In this work, we formulate adaptive WAM execution as a future-reality verification problem: the robot should execute longer when the WAM-predicted future remains reliable, and replan earlier when reality deviates from imagination. To this end, we propose Future Forward Dynamics Causal Attention (FFDC), a lightweight verifier that jointly reasons over predicted future actions, predicted visual dynamics, real observations, and language instructions to estimate whether the remaining action rollout can still be trusted. FFDC enables adaptive action chunk sizes as an emergent consequence of prediction-observation consistency, preserving the efficiency of long-horizon execution while restoring responsiveness in contact-rich or difficult phases. We further introduce Mixture-of-Horizon Training to improve long-horizon trajectory coverage for adaptive execution. Experiments on the RoboTwin benchmark and in the real world demonstrate that our method achieves a strong robustness-efficiency trade-off: on RoboTwin, it reduces WAM forward passes by 69.10% and execution time by 34.02%, while improving success rate by 2.54% over the short-chunk baseline; in real-world experiments, it improves success rate by 35%.

</details>


### [84] [RobotEQ: Transitioning from Passive Intelligence to Active Intelligence in Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06234)
*Kuofei Fang,Xinyi Che,Haomin Ouyang,Shufan Zhang,Xuehao Wang,Qi Liu,Liyi Liu,Chenqi Zhang,Wenxi Cai,Wenyu Dai,Jinyang Wu,Fan Zhang,Haoyu Chen,Bin He,Zheng Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RobotEQ，首个评估具身AI在无明确指令下理解并遵守社会规范的主动智能基准，包含1900张第一人称图像、5353个动作判断问题和1286个空间定位问题。实验表明现有模型在主动智能上表现不足，尤其是空间定位任务，而RAG技术可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身AI研究聚焦于根据显式指令完成任务，但机器人融入人类社会需要理解哪些行为被允许或禁止，即使没有明确命令。现有工作缺乏对主动智能（无需用户引导即可遵循社会规范）的评估基准。

Method: 构建RobotEQ-Data数据集（1900张第一人称图像，覆盖10个具身类别和56个子类别，包含5353个动作判断问题和1286个空间定位问题）；建立RobotEQ-Bench评估基准；采用RAG技术引入外部社会规范知识库提升模型性能。

Result: 当前最先进模型在主动智能任务上表现不足，尤其在空间定位方面；利用RAG技术引入外部社会规范知识库可普遍提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 这项工作可促进机器人从用户引导的被动操作向主动社会遵从的转变，为具身AI的社会规范理解提供了首个基准评估框架。

Abstract: Embodied AI is a prominent research topic in both academia and industry. Current research centers on completing tasks based on explicit user instructions. However, for robots to integrate into human society, they must understand which actions are permissible and which are prohibited, even without explicit commands. We refer to the user-guided AI as passive intelligence and the unguided AI as active intelligence. This paper introduces RobotEQ, the first benchmark for active intelligence, aiming to assess whether existing models can comprehend and adhere to social norms in embodied scenarios. First, we construct RobotEQ-Data, a dataset consisting of 1,900 egocentric images, spanning 10 representative embodied categories and 56 subcategories. Through extensive manual annotation, we provide 5,353 action judgment questions and 1,286 spatial grounding questions, specifying appropriate robot actions across diverse scenarios. Furthermore, we establish RobotEQ-Bench to evaluate the performance of state-of-the-art models on this task. Experimental results show that current models still fall short in achieving reliable active intelligence, particularly in spatial grounding. Meanwhile, we observe that leveraging RAG techniques to incorporate external social norm knowledge bases can generally enhance performance. This work can facilitate the transition of robotics from user-guided passive manipulation to active social compliance.

</details>


### [85] [CKT-WAM: Parameter-Efficient Context Knowledge Transfer Between World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06247)
*Yuhua Jiang,Yijun Guo,Hongbing Yang,Guojun Lei,Nuo Chen,Yinuo Zhang,Shaoqiang Yan,Bo Lin,Feifei Gao,Biqing Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CKT-WAM框架，通过在文本嵌入空间传递紧凑上下文知识，实现异构世界动作模型间的参数高效知识迁移，在LIBERO-Plus上以1.17%可训练参数达到86.1%成功率，并在真实世界长时程操作任务中取得83.3%平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构世界动作模型（WAMs）间知识迁移面临隐式接口不匹配、适应成本高以及传统蒸馏目标刚性等挑战，现有方法难以高效实现跨模型知识转移。

Method: CKT-WAM通过压缩器的可学习查询交叉注意力（LQCA）提取并压缩教师模型的中间隐层状态，利用常开通用适配器、轻量路由器和稀疏激活的专用适配器进行变换，将得到的紧凑上下文嵌入附加到学生模型的条件文本嵌入中，实现知识注入。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus上以1.17%可训练参数达到86.1%总成功率，零样本泛化性能一致提升；在真实世界四类多步长时程任务中取得83.3%平均成功率。

Conclusion: CKT-WAM通过文本嵌入空间的紧凑上下文知识迁移，避免了输出模仿和密集隐状态匹配，实现了参数高效且性能优越的异构WAM知识迁移，在仿真和真实世界任务中均表现优异。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) provide a powerful generative framework for embodied control, yet transferring knowledge across heterogeneous WAMs remains challenging due to mismatched latent interfaces, high adaptation cost, and the rigidity of conventional distillation objectives. We propose \textbf{CKT-WAM}, a parameter-efficient \textbf{C}ontext \textbf{K}nowledge \textbf{T}ransfer framework that transfers teacher WAM's knowledge into a student WAM through a compact context in the text embedding space, rather than output imitation or dense hidden-state matching. Specifically, CKT-WAM extracts intermediate teacher hidden states, reduces the number of tokens via compressors' learnable-query cross attention (LQCA), and transforms them through an always-on generalized adapter, a lightweight router, and sparsely activated specialized adapters. The resulting context is then appended to the student's conditioning textual embeddings, thereby injecting the transferred knowledge into the student with minimal architectural modification. Experiments show that CKT-WAM consistently improves zero-shot generalization and achieves the best overall performance on LIBERO-Plus, reaching 86.1\% total success rate with only 1.17\% trainable parameters, while approaching full fine-tuning performance. Beyond simulation, CKT-WAM also demonstrates strong real-world long-horizon manipulation ability, achieving the best average success rate of 83.3\% across four multi-step and long-horizon tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/YuhuaJiang2002/CKT-WAM.

</details>


### [86] [Toward Visually Realistic Simulation: A Benchmark for Evaluating Robot Manipulation in Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06311)
*Yixin Zhu,Zixiong Wang,Jian Yang,Jin Xie,Jingyi Yu,Jiayuan Gu,Beibei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VISER，一个基于物理渲染的高保真视觉基准，用于评估机器人操作策略。通过系统分析照明和材质对视觉真实感的影响，并利用多模态大语言模型自动化生成物理合理的3D资产，在多个任务上实现仿真与真实世界0.92的皮尔逊相关系数，有效弥合了仿真到真实的视觉域差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作仿真基准虽覆盖多种任务类别，但缺乏视觉真实感，导致仿真与真实世界之间存在巨大的域差距，削弱了基于仿真的评估在预测真实世界性能方面的可靠性。

Method: 首先系统分析照明和材质对几何推理和空间定位的影响，发现这些因素被现有基准严重忽视。然后构建VISER基准，包含超过1000个具有物理渲染材质的3D资产及高保真场景。提出自动化流程，利用多模态大语言模型进行材质感知的部件分割和材质检索，实现物理合理资产的可扩展生成。基于此构建抓取、放置、长时程等多样化评估任务。

Result: VISER基准在多种策略上实现了仿真与真实世界性能之间的强相关性，平均皮尔逊相关系数达到0.92。

Conclusion: 通过提升仿真环境的视觉真实感（特别是照明和材质），可以显著缩小仿真到真实的域差距，使仿真评估更可靠地预测真实世界中的机器人操作性能。

Abstract: Reliable simulation evaluation of robot manipulation policies serves as a high-fidelity proxy for real-world performance. Although existing benchmarks cover a wide range of task categories, they lack visual realism, creating a large domain gap between simulation and reality. This undermines the reliability of simulation-based evaluation in predicting real-world performance. To mitigate the sim-to-real visual gap, we conduct a systematic analysis to isolate the effects of lighting and material. Our results show that these factors play a critical role in geometric reasoning and spatial grounding, yet are largely overlooked in existing benchmarks. Motivated by the analysis, we propose VISER, a visually realistic benchmark for evaluating robot manipulation in simulation. VISER features a high-fidelity dataset of over 1,000 3D assets with physically-based rendering (PBR) materials, along with 3D scenes created from these assets through curated layouts or generation. To this end, we propose an automated pipeline leveraging Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) for material-aware part segmentation and material retrieval, enabling scalable generation of physically plausible assets. Building on the high-fidelity 3D asset dataset, we construct diverse evaluation tasks, such as grasping, placing, and long-horizon tasks, enabling scalable and reproducible assessment of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. Our benchmark shows a strong correlation between simulation and real-world performance, achieving an average Pearson correlation coefficient of 0.92 across different policies.

</details>


### [87] [AssistDLO: Assistive Teleoperation for Deformable Linear Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06323)
*Berk Guler,Simon Manschitz,Kay Pompetzki,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AssistDLO，一个结合多视图状态估计、视觉辅助和基于控制屏障函数的几何感知共享自主控制器的辅助遥操作框架，用于操控可变形线性物体（如绳索）。通过双手解结用户实验发现，SA-CBF对新手用户帮助最大（成功率从71%提升至88%），而专家用户偏好视觉辅助，且不同刚度和长度的绳索需要不同的辅助策略，表明有效遥操作需要自适应、用户感知和材料感知的共享自主控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形线性物体（DLO）因无限维构型空间和复杂非线性动力学而难以操控。遥操作中深度不确定性阻碍状态感知与反应，而传统共享自主方法依赖简单几何吸引子，无法保持DLO几何形状。

Method: 提出AssistDLO框架，包括实时多视图状态估计、视觉辅助（VA）、以及基于控制屏障函数的几何感知共享自主控制器（SA-CBF）。SA-CBF作为几何感知漏斗，在保留操作者高层权威的同时精确引导抓取。

Result: 通过双手解结用户实验（N=22，不同长度和刚度的绳索）评估。SA-CBF使新手成功率从71%提升至88%，对刚性绳索效果显著；专家用户偏好视觉辅助；高柔性长绳索更受益于视觉支持而非局部动作辅助。

Conclusion: 有效的DLO遥操作不能依赖固定策略，需要自适应、用户感知和材料感知的共享自主控制方法。

Abstract: Manipulating Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs) is challenging in robotics due to their infinite-dimensional configuration space and complex nonlinear dynamics. In teleoperation, depth uncertainty hinders state perception and reaction. AssistDLO addresses this challenge as an assistive teleoperation framework for DLO manipulation that combines real-time multi-view state estimation, visual assistance (VA), and a geometry-aware shared-autonomy controller based on Control Barrier Functions (SA-CBF). While traditional shared autonomy methods often rely on simple geometric attractors and may fail to preserve DLO geometry, SA-CBF acts as a geometry-aware funnel, facilitating precise grasping while preserving the operator's high-level authority. The framework is evaluated in a bimanual knot-untangling user study (N = 22) using ropes with varying length and rigidity. Results show that the effectiveness of the assistance depends strongly on operator expertise and DLO properties. SA-CBF provides the strongest gains for naive users, acting as a skill equalizer that increases task success from 71% to 88%, and is effective for stiffer ropes. Conversely, expert users prefer VA, and highly compliant, long ropes benefit more from visual support than localized action assistance. Ultimately, these findings demonstrate that effective DLO teleoperation cannot rely on a fixed strategy, highlighting the critical need for adaptive, user-aware, and material-aware shared autonomy.

</details>


### [88] [TouchDrive: Electronics-Free Tactile Sensing Interface for Assistive Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06432)
*Jing Xu,Xuezhi Niu,Didem Gurdur Broo,Klas Hjort*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TouchDrive是一种低成本、无电子元件的触觉传感接口，通过气动阀将接触力直接转化为触觉反馈，帮助用户调节抓取力，实现对易碎和柔性物体的精确操作，已在多种机器人平台验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辅助抓取系统依赖电子传感和多阶段处理流程，系统复杂度高且可及性差，限制了在辅助操作场景中的广泛应用。

Method: 采用气动常闭阀、压缩空气罐、传感元件和触觉反馈执行器组成的纯机械回路，将接触力通过阀门切换直接转化为气动反馈，无需任何电子元件。

Result: 在多种机器人平台上验证，能可靠处理包括水果和日常物品在内的多达20种不同物体，实现精确且稳健的柔性抓取。

Conclusion: TouchDrive展示了无电子触觉传感在辅助抓取中的实用价值，有望提升系统的可及性和可靠性，降低复杂度和成本。

Abstract: Assistive robotic grasping plays an important role in enabling safe and adaptive manipulation of diverse objects. However, existing systems often rely on electronic sensing and multi-stage processing pipelines, increasing system complexity and reducing accessibility. To address these limitations, we present TouchDrive, a cost-effective, electronics-free tactile sensing interface for assistive grasping. TouchDrive directly converts contact forces into pneumatic feedback through valve-mediated switching, integrating sensing, signal generation, and feedback within a single passive mechanical loop. The system can be employed using a pneumatic normally closed valve, a compressed air tank, sensing element, and haptic feedback actuator without electronics. By delivering tactile cues, TouchDrive empowers users to modulate grasp forces, enabling precise and robust delicate manipulation of compliant and fragile objects. The interface has been validated across diverse robotic platforms, consistently demonstrating reliable performance and practical applicability in assistive grasping tasks, such as handling fruits and everyday items (up to 20 objects).

</details>


### [89] [GA3T: A Ground-Aerial Terrain Traversability Dataset for Heterogeneous Robot Teams in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06478)
*Siwei Cai,Knut Peterson,Quan Tran,Christian Ricks,Dhanush Parthasarathy,Amir Kaidarov,Neil Deshpande,Sukaina Najm,David Han,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GA3T是一个真实世界多机器人协同感知数据集，由地面无人车（Clearpath Husky）和空中无人机（Autel EVO II）在森林小径、岩石路、泥地、雪地和草地等非结构化地形中采集，包含13000+同步帧、8000+手动标注图像，支持跨视角融合、可通行性估计和协同场景理解研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构空地机器人团队能通过互补感知提升复杂环境感知能力，但缺乏真实世界中多机器人协同感知数据集，现有数据集多聚焦于SLAM或仿真协同驾驶，缺少非结构化地形下的多模态重叠观测数据。

Method: 使用Clearpath Husky UGV（搭载3D LiDAR、立体相机、IMU、GPS）和Autel EVO II UAV（搭载RGB相机、热红外相机、GPS）在4种非结构化环境（森林小径、岩石路、泥地、雪地、草地）中同步采集数据，包含13000+同步帧（约29分钟），并提供SAM 3-based零样本分割和8000+手动标注图像。数据集采集于早春，稀疏树冠允许无人机部分观测地面机器人和地形。

Result: 生成了包含13000+同步帧、8000+手动标注图像的多机器人协同感知数据集，覆盖4种不同非结构化环境，提供跨模态（LiDAR、RGB、热红外、IMU、GPS）和跨视角（地面-空中）的丰富观测数据。

Conclusion: GA3T数据集填补了真实世界非结构化环境下空地机器人协同感知数据集的空白，特别适用于跨视角感知、空地视角融合、可通行性估计和协同场景理解等研究，其早春采集的稀疏树冠场景为遮挡感知协同感知提供了独特价值。

Abstract: Heterogeneous air-ground robot teams combine complementary sensing modalities, mobility characteristics, and spatial viewpoints that can significantly enhance perception in complex outdoor environments. However, progress in multi-robot collaborative perception has been constrained by the lack of real-world datasets featuring overlapping multi-modal observations from platforms operating in unstructured terrain. We present GA3T (Ground-Aerial Team for Terrain Traversal), a real-world multi-robot collaborative perception dataset collected using a Clearpath Husky UGV and an Autel EVO~II UAV across diverse unstructured environments, including forest trails, rocky paths, muddy terrain, snow piles, and grass-covered fields. The ground platform provides 3D LiDAR, stereo camera, IMU, and GPS data, while the aerial platform contributes RGB imagery, thermal/infrared observations, and GPS from a complementary overhead viewpoint, allowing for rich cross-modal and cross-view perception. The dataset is collected in 4 unique environments, with over 13,000 synchronized frames across approximately 29 minutes of operation, and includes both SAM~3-based zero-shot segmentation and over 8,000 manually labeled images. A unique aspect of the dataset is its early-spring collection period, during which sparse tree canopies allow the aerial robot to partially observe the ground robot and terrain through the trees, allowing for occlusion-aware collaborative perception. Unlike prior multi-robot datasets that focus on SLAM or simulated cooperative driving, GA3T is specifically designed to support research on cross-view perception, air-ground viewpoint fusion, traversability estimation, and collaborative scene understanding in real off-road environments.

</details>


### [90] [OA-WAM: Object-Addressable World Action Model for Robust Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06481)
*Yushan Liu,Peibo Sun,Shoujie Li,Yifan Xie,Lingfeng Zhang,Xintao Chao,Shiyuan Dong,Fang Chen,Xiao-Ping Zhang,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OA-WAM通过将场景分解为可寻址的物体槽位和机器人槽位，实现了鲁棒的机器人操作，在LIBERO上达到97.8%的准确率，且在场景扰动下的物体交换绑定余弦达到0.87，远超整体式方法的0.09。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）将预测的世界表示为整体图像、视频令牌或全局潜在变量，这些表示方式在指令指向特定物体时难以被动作解码器处理，尤其在场景移位下物体身份与上下文纠缠不清。

Method: 将每一帧分解为N+1个槽位状态（1个机器人槽位+N个物体槽位），每个槽位包含持久化地址向量和时变内容向量；采用块因果序列与文本、图像、本体感知和过去动作令牌融合；世界头预测下一帧槽位状态，流匹配动作头解码16步连续动作块；通过地址键路由跨槽位注意力，并在每层Transformer重置地址切片，实现寻址能力。

Result: LIBERO上97.8%，SimplerEnv上79.3%，在LIBERO-Plus几何轴上达到SOTA，七轴聚合保持竞争力；因果槽干预测试中交换绑定余弦为0.87，整体式基线最高仅0.09。

Conclusion: 可寻址的物体状态为场景扰动下的鲁棒世界-动作建模提供了有效的接口。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enhance Vision-Language-Action policies by jointly predicting scene evolution and robot actions, but existing methods usually represent the predicted world as holistic images, video tokens, or global latents. These representations are difficult for an action decoder to address when an instruction refers to a particular object, especially under scene shifts where object identity is entangled with context. We propose OA-WAM, an Object-Addressable World Action Model for robust robot manipulation. OA-WAM decomposes each frame into N+1 slot states, with one robot slot and N object slots. Each slot contains a persistent address vector and a time-varying content vector, and is fused with text, image, proprioception, and past-action tokens in a block-causal sequence. A world head predicts next-frame slot states, while a flow-matching action head decodes a 16-step continuous action chunk in the same forward pass. Addressability is enforced by routing cross-slot attention through address-only keys and resetting the address slice at every transformer layer, separating which object to act on from what that object currently is without adding extra tokens. OA-WAM matches strong VLA and WAM baselines on LIBERO (97.8%) and SimplerEnv (79.3%), reaches state-of-the-art performance on the most relevant LIBERO-Plus geometric axes, and remains competitive on the seven-axis aggregate. A causal slot-intervention test yields a swap-binding cosine of 0.87, versus at most 0.09 for holistic baselines. These results suggest that addressable object states provide an effective interface for robust world-action modeling under scene perturbations.

</details>


### [91] [Lie Group Formulation of Recursive Dynamics Algorithms of Higher Order for Floating-Base Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06498)
*Ahmed Ali,Chiara Gabellieri,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了浮基树形机器人系统（基座位姿在SE(3)上演化，附机构型在T^{n1} × R^{n2}流形上）的李群牛顿-欧拉算法、体惯量算法和混合动力学算法的高阶时间导数计算流程，推导了封闭形式运动方程，并证明了惯性张量在各阶导数下保持不变。在12自由度空中机械臂上的数值仿真验证了最高五阶动力学，且计算复杂度关于导数阶次呈二次增长，而自动微分基线呈指数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人动力学算法通常只提供零阶动力学计算，但控制器设计、轨迹优化和系统辨识等任务需要动力学的高阶时间导数。现有自动微分方法在处理高阶导数时计算成本呈指数增长，亟需高效的结构化算法。

Method: 基于李群框架，采用空间运动旋量表示，分别推导了浮基树形系统的牛顿-欧拉算法、体惯量算法和混合动力学算法的递归高阶时间导数；从递归形式中提取出封闭形式的运动方程，识别出满足无源性条件的科里奥利矩阵，并证明了体惯量张量在各阶导数下保持不变。

Result: 给出了12自由度空中机械臂的几何正动力学和逆动力学的解析表达式及其一阶导数，数值仿真成功评估了高达五阶的动力学。计算复杂度分析表明，所提方法的计算成本随导数阶次呈O(k²)增长，而自动微分基线呈指数增长。

Conclusion: 所提出的高阶动力学导数计算方法在效率上显著优于自动微分，为机器人控制、轨迹优化等需要高阶动力学信息的应用提供了高效的理论工具和算法实现。

Abstract: In this paper, we describe procedures for computing higher-order time derivatives of the Lie-group Newton-Euler, Articulated-Body Inertia, and hybrid dynamics algorithms for floating-base trees, where the base configuration evolves on SE(3) and the attached mechanism is an open kinematic tree with configuration on the (n1+n2)-dimensional manifold T^{n1} \times R^{n2}, using spatial representation of twists. After presenting the algorithms, we collect the resulting recursions into closed-form equations of motion, identifying an admissible Coriolis matrix satisfying the passivity property, and showing that the articulated inertia tensor remains unchanged across all time derivatives. We then apply the developed methods to a 12-DoF aerial manipulator to derive analytical expressions for its geometric forward and inverse dynamics along with their first time derivatives whereas the numerical simulations successfully evaluate these dynamics up to fifth order. Finally, to demonstrate their practical utility, we benchmark the proposed extensions and show that, in the considered tests, their computational cost scales quadratically with the derivative order, whereas the automatic-differentiation baseline exhibits exponential scaling.

</details>


### [92] [ReActor: Reinforcement Learning for Physics-Aware Motion Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06593)
*David Müller,Agon Serifi,Sammy Christen,Ruben Grandia,Espen Knoop,Moritz Bächer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种双层优化框架，将人体运动重新定位到机器人形态上，同时通过强化学习训练跟踪策略，生成物理一致的运动，并成功在四足机器人等不同形态上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在将人体运动重定位到机器人时会产生物理不一致（如脚滑、自碰撞、动态不可行运动），阻碍了下游的模仿学习。

Method: 提出双层优化框架：上层优化自适应参考运动到机器人形态，下层通过强化学习训练跟踪策略；通过推导上层损失的近似梯度使优化可行；仅需稀疏的语义刚体对应关系，并自动识别最优参数化值。

Result: 在仿真和硬件上验证了该方法，成功将人体运动重定位到与人体差异很大的形态（包括四足机器人），并产生了物理合理的运动。

Conclusion: 该方法通过将重定位与物理仿真直接集成，无需手动调参，能够生成物理一致的运动，促进鲁棒的模仿学习，适用于形态差异大的机器人。

Abstract: Retargeting human kinematic reference motion onto a robot's morphology remains a formidable challenge. Existing methods often produce physical inconsistencies, such as foot sliding, self-collisions, or dynamically infeasible motions, which hinder downstream imitation learning. We propose a bilevel optimization framework that jointly adapts reference motions to a robot's morphology while training a tracking policy using reinforcement learning. To make the optimization tractable, we derive an approximate gradient for the upper-level loss. Our framework requires only a sparse set of semantic rigid-body correspondences and eliminates the need for manual tuning by identifying optimal values for a parameterization expressive enough to preserve characteristic motion across different embodiments. Moreover, by integrating retargeting directly with physics simulation, we produce physically plausible motions that facilitate robust imitation learning. We validate our method in simulation and on hardware, demonstrating challenging motions for morphologies that differ significantly from a human, including retargeting onto a quadruped.

</details>


### [93] [Cross-Modal Navigation with Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06595)
*Shuo Liu,Xinzichen Li,Christopher Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CRONA框架，利用多智能体强化学习实现跨模态导航，通过控制相关的辅助信念和集中式多模态评论家提升协作效率。实验表明，多智能体方法在视觉-声学导航任务中显著优于单智能体基线，并分析了不同场景下同质与异质协作的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态数据难以获取高质量对齐样本，且训练单一大模型面临表征复杂、策略空间大的挑战，因此需要一种可扩展的跨模态协作范式。

Method: 提出CRONA框架，基于多智能体强化学习，利用控制相关的辅助信念（auxiliary beliefs）和带全局状态的集中式多模态评论家（centralized multi-modal critic）来改善轻量级模态专用智能体之间的协作。

Result: 在视觉-声学导航任务上，多智能体方法在性能和效率上显著优于单智能体基线；同质协作适用于短程显著线索导航，异质协作高效且通用，复杂大场景需更强多模态感知和更大模型容量。

Conclusion: 跨模态多智能体协作是可扩展且有效的导航范式，不同场景下同质与异质协作各有优势，且复杂场景对感知和模型容量有更高需求。

Abstract: Robust embodied navigation relies on complementary sensory cues. However, high-quality and well-aligned multi-modal data is often difficult to obtain in practice. Training a monolithic model is also challenging as rich multi-modal inputs induce complex representations and substantially enlarge the policy space. Cross-modal collaboration among lightweight modality-specialized agents offers a scalable paradigm. It enables flexible deployment and parallel execution, while preserving the strength of each modality. In this paper, we propose \textbf{CRONA}, a Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework for \textbf{Cro}ss-Modal \textbf{Na}vigation. CRONA improves collaboration by leveraging control-relevant auxiliary beliefs and a centralized multi-modal critic with global state. Experiments on visual-acoustic navigation tasks show that multi-agent methods significantly improve performance and efficiency over single-agent baselines. We find that homogeneous collaboration with limited modalities is sufficient for short-range navigation under salient cues; heterogeneous collaboration among agents with complementary modalities is generally efficient and effective; and navigation in large, complex environments requires both richer multi-modal perception and increased model capacity.

</details>


### [94] [Multi-Robot Coordination in V2X Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06662)
*John Pravin Arockiasamy,Alexey Vinel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向社交机器人的V2X通信框架，包含Robot Awareness Service (RAS)和Robot Maneuver Coordination Service (RMCS)两个设施层服务，实现机器人在复杂城市交通中的去中心化协作与弱势道路使用者（VRU）整合，并通过实物验证与仿真评估证明了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2X通信标准主要面向车辆，缺乏对社交机器人（如人形机器人、四足机器人）在交通环境中进行角色感知、任务导向的协作意识与机动协调的支持，且无法有效整合不具备V2X通信能力的弱势道路使用者（如行人）。

Method: 基于ETSI Cooperative Awareness和Maneuver Coordination服务，提出RAS和RMCS两个设施层服务及其对应的消息格式（RAM和RMCM）。RAS支持角色感知、任务导向的机器人意识并整合外部检测的VRU；RMCS支持事件驱动、低延迟的机器人机动协调。通过有限状态机建模协调过程，并在真实场景中由人形机器人与四足机器人协助行人过马路进行验证，同时开展仿真评估VRU聚类性能。

Result: 实物验证实现了双机器人协助行人过马路的确定性多机器人协调；仿真表明RAS聚类能够有效整合非V2X VRU进入安全关键区域，同时减少V2X VRU的冗余传输，降低信道负载。

Conclusion: 提出的RAS和RMCS服务为将协作机器人集成到未来互联、协作与自动化出行（CCAM）生态系统提供了可扩展且符合标准的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) communication framework that enables decentralized cooperation among social robots operating in complex urban traffic environments. Building on ETSI Cooperative Awareness and Maneuver Coordination services, the framework introduces two robot-centric facility-layer services: the Robot Awareness Service (RAS) and the Robot Maneuver Coordination Service (RMCS), realized through the Robot Awareness Message (RAM) and the Robot Maneuver Coordination Message (RMCM), respectively. RAS enables role-aware, task-oriented robot awareness while integrating externally detected Vulnerable Road Users (VRUs), including non-V2X pedestrians, into cooperative awareness. RMCS supports event-driven, low-latency coordination of robot maneuvers under explicitly established roles, without centralized infrastructure or prior pairing. A real-world proof of concept demonstrates deterministic multi-robot coordination between a humanoid robot and a quadrupedal robot assisting a pedestrian during a road-crossing scenario, governed by a formally specified finite-state coordination model. Complementary simulations evaluate robot-mediated VRU clustering in mixed V2X environments, showing that RAS-based clustering integrates non-V2X VRUs in safety-critical areas while reducing redundant transmissions from V2X-enabled VRUs, thereby lowering channel load. Together, the proposed services provide a scalable and standards-aligned foundation for integrating cooperative robots into future Connected, Cooperative, and Automated Mobility ecosystems.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Nationwide EHR-Based Chronic Rhinosinusitis Prediction Using Demographic-Stratified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05213)
*Sicong Chang,Yidan Shen,Justina Varghese,Akshay R Prabhakar,Sebastian Guadarrama-Sistos-Vazquez,Jiefu Chen,Masayoshi Takashima,Omar G. Ahmed,Renjie Hu,Xin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用《All of Us》全国纵向EHR数据，通过混合特征选择管道和人口统计学分层模型，在诊断前两年预测慢性鼻窦炎（CRS），总体AUC达0.8461，较最佳基线提升0.0168，证明常规EHR数据可支持人群代表性CRS风险分层并指导初级保健中的早期分诊与转诊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CRS是一种常见异质性炎症性疾病，早期识别困难，症状与过敏性鼻炎等重叠，且表型异质性掩盖风险模式；既往预测研究多依赖单一机构队列，缺乏人群水平普适性。

Method: 利用《All of Us》全国纵向EHR数据，以诊断前两年病史预测CRS；采用混合特征选择管道（基于患病率的统计筛选+基于模型的重要性排序），将约11万个候选编码压缩为100个可解释特征；按性别和生命阶段训练6个人口统计学分层模型，并针对各亚组进行超参数调优。

Result: 总体AUC达0.8461，较最佳基线提升0.0168。

Conclusion: 常规收集的EHR数据可支持具有人群代表性的CRS风险分层，有助于初级保健中更早的分诊和转诊优先级排序。

Abstract: Chronic rhinosinusitis (CRS) is a common heterogeneous inflammatory disorder that causes substantial morbidity and healthcare costs. CRS is difficult to identify early from routine encounters, as symptom presentations overlap with common conditions such as allergic rhinitis, and heterogeneous phenotypes further obscure risk patterns. Prior predictive studies often rely on single-institutional cohorts , which reduce population-level generalizability. To overcome this, we leveraged nationwide longitudinal EHR data from the \textit{All of Us} Research Program to predict CRS diagnosis using two years of pre-diagnostic history. To address extreme feature sparsity and dimensionality in coded EHR data, we implemented a hybrid feature-selection pipeline that combines prevalence-based statistical screening with model-based importance ranking, compressing approximately 110,000 candidate codes into 100 interpretable features. To capture demographic heterogeneity, we trained demographic stratified models across six adult sex and life-stage subgroups with subgroup-specific hyperparameter tuning. Our framework achieved an overall AUC of 0.8461, improving discrimination by 0.0168 over the best baseline. These results demonstrate that routinely collected EHR data may support population-representative CRS risk stratification and inform earlier triage and referral prioritization in primary care.

</details>


### [96] [Optimal Contextual Pricing under Agnostic Non-Lipschitz Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05609)
*Jianyu Xu,Yu-Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对线性估值、有界支撑且不可知的非Lipschitz噪声下的上下文动态定价问题，提出Conservative-Markdown Redirect-UCB Pricing算法，通过随机参数估计、保守残差网格探测和基于置信度的单步重定向，实现了$\tilde O(T^{2/3})$最优遗憾，匹配下界并改进了此前$\tilde O(T^{3/4})$的上界，在随机良态上下文下闭合了长期存在的遗憾差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文动态定价算法依赖需求曲线的光滑性进行跨上下文插值，但在非Lipschitz噪声（含任意跳跃和原子分布）下该假设不成立，导致遗憾性能退化。此前最优方法仅能达到$\tilde O(T^{3/4})$遗憾，与已知$\tilde O(T^{2/3})$下界之间存在未闭合的间隙，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 提出Conservative-Markdown Redirect-UCB Pricing算法，包含三个核心组件：(1) 随机参数估计：以随机化方式估计线性估值参数；(2) 保守残差网格探测：构建残差网格进行保守探测以应对非光滑需求曲线；(3) 基于置信度的单步重定向：利用置信区间进行单步定价调整，平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在理论上实现了$\tilde O(T^{2/3})$遗憾上界，匹配Kleinberg和Leighton (2003)给出的已知下界（仅差对数因子），显著改进了Xu和Wang (2022)的$\tilde O(T^{3/4})$上界，证明了该界的最优性。

Conclusion: 该工作在线性估值上下文动态定价中，针对非Lipschitz不可知噪声分布闭合了长期存在的遗憾差距，证明了$\tilde O(T^{2/3})$是最优遗憾界，算法为多项式时间可执行。

Abstract: We study contextual dynamic pricing with linear valuations and bounded-support agnostic noise, whose induced demand curve may be non-Lipschitz with arbitrary jumps and atoms. Such discontinuities break the cross-context interpolation arguments used by smooth-demand pricing algorithms, while the best previous method achieved only $\tilde O(T^{3/4})$ regret. We propose Conservative-Markdown Redirect-UCB Pricing, a polynomial-time algorithm that combines randomized parameter estimation, conservative residual-grid probing, and confidence-based one-step redirection. Our algorithm achieves $\tilde O(T^{2/3})$ optimal regret, matching the known lower bounds of Kleinberg and Leighton (2003) up to logarithmic factors and improving over the previous upper bound of Xu and Wang (2022). Under stochastic well-conditioned contexts, this closes the long-existing open regret gap in linear-valuation contextual pricing under agnostic non-Lipschitz noise distribution.

</details>


### [97] [SAT: Sequential Agent Tuning for Coordinator Free Plug and Play Multi-LLM Training with Monotonic Improvement Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05216)
*Yi Xie,Yangyang Xu,Yi Fan,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出顺序智能体微调（SAT），一种无协调器的多智能体训练范式，通过因子化策略和块坐标更新实现多个小模型的协作训练，保证单调改进和即插即用不变性。三个4B智能体组成的团队（总计12B）在AIME24/25基准上平均超越Qwen3-32B达3.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型参数量大、部署成本高，而现有利用多个小模型协作的方法面临联合更新导致的分布偏移和训练不稳定问题。

Method: 提出SAT框架：将团队表示为因子化策略，进行逐智能体的块坐标更新；开发序列感知的、基于当前团队策略的on-policy优势估计器；为每个智能体设置KL信任域以隔离占据漂移。理论上证明单调改进和即插即用不变性（任意智能体可升级为更强模型而无需重新训练团队）。

Result: 三个4B智能体（总计12B）在AIME24/25上平均超越Qwen3-32B达3.9%；替换两个智能体为8B版本后，综合分数提升10.4%。

Conclusion: SAT提供了一种可扩展、去中心化的多智能体训练方案，兼顾理论保证与实证性能，支持灵活升级智能体。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) with a large number of parameters achieve strong performance but are often prohibitively expensive to deploy. Recent work explores using teams of smaller, more efficient LLMs that collectively match or even outperform a single large model. However, jointly updating multiple agents introduces compounding distribution shifts, making coordination and stability during training difficult. We address this by introducing Sequential Agent Tuning (SAT), a coordinator-free training paradigm. SAT represents the team as a factorized policy and employs block-coordinate updates over agents, enabling scalable, decentralized training without a central controller. Specifically, we develop a sequence-aware, on-policy advantage estimator that conditions on the evolving team policy, coupled with per-agent KL trust regions that isolate occupancy drift. Theoretically, this framework provides two critical guarantees. First, it ensures monotonic improvement, stabilizing the training process. Second, it establishes provable plug-and-play invariance: any agent can be upgraded to a stronger model without retraining the rest of the team, with a formal guarantee that the performance bound improves. Empirically, a team of three 4B agents (12B total) trained with SAT surpasses the much larger Qwen3-32B on AIME24/25 benchmarks by 3.9\% on average. We validate our plug-and-play theory by swapping in two 8B agents, which boosts the composite score by 10.4\%. We provide code and appendix of proof at https://github.com/Yydc/SAT-AAMAS

</details>


### [98] [Feature Dimensionality Outweighs Model Complexity in Breast Cancer Subtype Classification Using TCGA-BRCA Gene Expression Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06562)
*Meena Al Hasani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用TCGA-BRCA基因表达数据，评估了模型复杂度（逻辑回归、随机森林、SVM）和特征选择（可变基因数量从50到20,518）对乳腺癌亚型分类性能的影响。结果表明：所有模型均获得高准确率，但宏F1分数揭示了亚型层面显著差异；逻辑回归在亚型间表现最稳定均衡，包括对稀有类别的检测；随机森林尽管整体准确率高但少数亚型表现不佳；SVM对特征维度敏感。研究强调了模型简洁性、评估指标和特征选择在高维生物分类任务中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因表达数据具有高维度和样本量有限的特点，给机器学习模型带来挑战。准确分类乳腺癌亚型对诊断和治疗选择至关重要，但现有研究缺乏对模型复杂度和特征选择对亚型分类（特别是稀有亚型）影响的系统评估。

Method: 使用TCGA-BRCA基因表达数据，训练逻辑回归、随机森林和支持向量机（SVM）三种模型，采用不同数量的高变异基因（50到20,518个）进行特征选择，通过分层5折交叉验证，使用准确率和宏F1分数评估性能。

Result: 所有模型均获得高准确率，但宏F1分析显示亚型层面性能存在显著差异。逻辑回归在各类亚型（包括稀有类别）中表现最稳定均衡；随机森林整体准确率高但在少数亚型上表现欠佳；SVM对特征维度数量敏感。

Conclusion: 模型简洁性、评估指标选择和特征选择在高维生物分类任务中至关重要。逻辑回归因其简单性更适合不平衡的基因表达数据分类，而宏F1分数比准确率更能反映模型在稀有亚型上的真实表现。

Abstract: Accurate classification of breast cancer subtypes from gene expression data is critical for diagnosis and treatment selection. However, such datasets are characterized by high dimensionality and limited sample size, posing challenges for machine learning models.
  In this study, we evaluate the impact of model complexity and feature selection on subtype classification performance using TCGA-BRCA gene expression data. Logistic regression, random forest, and support vector machine (SVM) models were trained using varying numbers of highly variable genes (50 to 20,518). Performance was evaluated using stratified 5-fold cross-validation and assessed with accuracy and macro F1 score. While all models achieved high accuracy, macro F1 analysis revealed substantial differences in subtype-level performance. Logistic regression demonstrated the most stable and balanced performance across subtypes, including improved detection of rare classes. Random forest underperformed on minority subtypes despite strong overall accuracy, while SVM showed sensitivity to feature dimensionality. These findings highlight the importance of model simplicity, evaluation metrics, and feature selection in high-dimensional biological classification tasks.

</details>


### [99] [Horizon-Constrained Rashomon Sets for Chaotic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05218)
*Gauri Kale,Rahul Vishwakarma,Holly Diamond,Ava Hedayatipour,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了"horizon-constrained Rashomon sets"框架，首次建立了混沌动力学与预测多重性之间的严格理论联系，证明混沌系统中Rashomon集合随预测时域指数收缩，并开发了基于下游效用的决策对齐模型选择算法，在合成和实际混沌系统中将决策质量提升18-34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管预测多重性（Rashomon效应）和混沌动力学都是机器学习中的基础挑战，但两者长期以来独立发展。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟，建立它们之间的理论联系，以指导在安全关键混沌领域中部署机器学习模型。

Method: 1) 提出horizon-constrained Rashomon sets理论框架，刻画混沌系统中模型多重性随预测时域的演化；2) 证明有效Rashomon集合以最大Lyapunov指数决定的速率指数收缩；3) 引入Lyapunov加权度量，提供预测分歧的更紧界；4) 开发基于下游效用的决策对齐模型选择算法。

Result: 在Lorenz-96、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky等合成混沌系统以及风电、交通、天气等实际应用中，所提框架在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，将决策质量提升18-34%。

Conclusion: 本文首次建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间的严格联系，为在安全关键混沌领域部署机器学习提供了原则性指导。

Abstract: Predictive multiplicity and chaotic dynamics represent two fundamental challenges in machine learning that have evolved independently despite their conceptual connections. We bridge this gap by introducing horizon-constrained Rashomon sets, a theoretical framework that characterizes how model multiplicity evolves with prediction horizon in chaotic systems. Unlike static prediction tasks where the Rashomon set remains fixed, chaos induces exponential divergence among initially similar models, fundamentally transforming the nature of predictive equivalence. We prove that the effective Rashomon set contracts exponentially with lead time at a rate determined by the maximum Lyapunov exponent and introduce Lyapunov-weighted metrics that provide tighter bounds on predictive disagreement. Leveraging these insights, we develop decision-aligned selection algorithms that choose among near-optimal models based on downstream utility rather than forecast accuracy alone. Extensive experiments on synthetic chaotic systems (Lorenz-96, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky) and real-world applications (wind power, traffic, weather) demonstrate that our framework improves decision quality by 18-34\% while maintaining competitive predictive performance. This work establishes the first rigorous connection between chaos theory and predictive multiplicity, providing principled guidance for deploying machine learning in safety-critical chaotic domains.

</details>


### [100] [MidSteer: Optimal Affine Framework for Steering Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05220)
*Tatiana Gaintseva,Andrew Stepanov,Ziquan Liu,Martin Benning,Gregory Slabaugh,Jiankang Deng,Ismail Elezi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出概念操控的形式化理论框架，建立操控与概念擦除的联系，提出LEACE-Switch和MidSteer方法，并在多种模型上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的中间表示操控方法（如概念 steering）在经验上取得了成功，但缺乏全面的理论框架，限制了其泛化性和可解释性。

Method: 1）建立steering与仿射概念擦除（LEACE）的理论联系；2）提出LEACE-Switch框架用于概念切换；3）提出MidSteer（最小扰动概念操控），一个更一般的仿射框架，放宽了假设条件，支持有向的最小扰动变换。

Result: MidSteer在视觉扩散模型和大语言模型等多个任务、模态和架构上表现优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文为概念steering提供了统一的理论基础，并提出了更通用的MidSteer方法，在保持最小扰动的同时实现了灵活的概念操控。

Abstract: Steering intermediate representations has emerged as a powerful strategy for controlling generative models, particularly in post-deployment alignment and safety settings. However, despite its empirical success, it currently lacks a comprehensive theoretical framework. In this paper, we bridge this gap by formalizing the theory of concept steering. First, we establish a link between steering and affine concept erasure, proving that the standard approach for removing unwanted behaviors is a special case of LEACE (a closed-form method for affine erasure). Next, we formulate a principled theoretical framework for concept switching, LEACE-Switch, and characterize the assumptions under which it provides an optimal affine solution. Building on this analysis, we then introduce MidSteer (Minimal Disturbance concept Steering), a more general affine framework for concept manipulation that relaxes these assumptions and enables directed, minimal-disturbance transformations. We demonstrate that MidSteer performs favorably across a range of tasks, modalities, and architectures, including vision diffusion models and large language models.

</details>


### [101] [Adaptive Computation Depth via Learned Token Routing in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05222)
*Ahmed Abdelmuniem Abdalla Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TSA是一种可学习的逐token门控机制，通过轻量级MLP为每个token生成连续暂停概率，实现端到端可微的层间动态路由，在字符级语言建模任务中以1.7%参数开销节省14-23%的token层计算，且无需显式深度正则化即可学习到与难度成比例的路由策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer对所有token使用相同数量的层，忽略了不同token的上下文难度差异，导致计算资源浪费。本文旨在设计一种能根据token难度动态调整计算深度的机制，提升计算效率。

Method: 提出Token-Selective Attention (TSA)，在相邻Transformer块之间引入可学习的逐token门控。每个门控由轻量级两层MLP构成，输出连续暂停概率，使整个机制端到端可微。仅增加1.7%参数，不改动基础架构。训练时不施加深度正则化（λ=0），任务损失梯度即可驱动路由器跳过20%的token-层操作。

Result: 在字符级语言建模任务中，Tiny-Shakespeare和enwik8上TSA节省14-23%的token层操作（TLOps），质量损失低于0.5%。在相同效率下，TSA的验证损失比early exit低0.7%。学习到的路由策略可直接迁移至推理阶段的稀疏执行，实现实际墙钟加速。

Conclusion: TSA证明了无需显式深度正则化，任务损失梯度本身即可驱动模型学习与token难度成比例的动态路由策略，在极小参数开销下实现显著的计算节省，且路由策略具有良好的泛化性和实际加速效果。

Abstract: Standard transformer architectures apply the same number of layers to every token regardless of contextual difficulty. We present Token-Selective Attention (TSA), a learned per-token gate on residual updates between consecutive transformer blocks. Each gate is a lightweight two-layer multi-layer perceptron (MLP) that produces a continuous halting probability, making the mechanism end-to-end differentiable with 1.7% parameter overhead and no changes to the base architecture. Notably, TSA learns difficulty-proportional routing without any explicit depth pressure: even at $λ=0$ (no depth regularisation), the task-loss gradient alone drives the router to skip 20% of token-layer operations. On character-level language modeling, TSA saved 14-23% of token-layer operations (TLOps) across Tiny-Shakespeare and enwik8 at <0.5% quality loss. At matched efficiency, TSA achieved 0.7% lower validation loss than early exit, and the learned routing transfers directly to inference-time sparse execution for real wall-clock speedup.

</details>


### [102] [Structural Instability of Feature Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05223)
*Yunpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀疏自编码器（SAE）中组合激活（compositional steering）的不稳定性，在高维稀疏锥流形几何框架下推导了组合崩溃阈值，发现ReLU整流在高压制区域会将微观波动累积为系统性漂移，产生类似棘轮效应的干扰增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管SAE在特征解耦和激活控制方面表现优异，但组合激活（同时激活多个不同语义潜在变量）的理论基础尚未被充分探索。主流的线性表示假说忽略了过完备字典中非线性干扰效应。

Method: 将激活空间建模为高维稀疏锥流形，在球面字典模型下推导组合崩溃渐近阈值（用信号锥的高斯平均宽度/统计维度刻画）。分析高偏置区域中ReLU整流如何将微观相关诱导的方差波动转化为系统性漂移。

Result: 在CLEVR结构语义特征上验证了预测的缩放趋势，层级相关性会加速转换过程。干扰增长符合棘轮效应特征。

Conclusion: 揭示了基于组合的激活控制在可扩展性上的几何约束，表明需要设计能显式管理干扰的组合机制，而非依赖朴素的线性叠加。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for disentangling feature superposition in transformer-based architectures, enabling precise control via activation steering. However, the theoretical foundations of compositional steering -- the simultaneous activation of distinct semantic latents -- remain under-explored. The prevailing Linear Representation Hypothesis often abstracts away non-linear interference effects that arise in overcomplete dictionaries. We present a geometric framework for analyzing the instability of feature unions. Modeling the activation space as a high-dimensional sparse cone manifold, we derive an asymptotic compositional-collapse threshold under a spherical dictionary model, characterized by the Gaussian mean width (statistical dimension) of the signal cone. We further show that, in the high-bias regime, ReLU rectification converts microscopic correlation-induced variance fluctuations into a systematic drift that accumulates under composition, yielding interference growth consistent with a ratchet effect. We validate the predicted scaling trends on structured semantic features extracted from CLEVR, where hierarchical correlations accelerate the transition relative to random baselines. Together, our results highlight geometric constraints on the scalability of union-based steering and motivate composition mechanisms that explicitly manage interference beyond naive linear superposition.

</details>


### [103] [Channel-Level Semantic Perturbations: Unlearnable Examples for Diverse Training Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05224)
*Bo Wang,Jia Ni,Mengnan Zhao,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了不可学习示例在预训练-微调范式下的表现，发现冻结预训练权重会显著削弱现有UE方法的效果，并提出了一种分层欺骗策略SSC以增强数据在PF范式下的不可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不可学习示例研究主要评估从头训练场景，而在广泛采用的预训练-微调范式下的行为尚未被探索，存在研究空白。

Method: 通过分析PF范式下冻结浅层保留语义、过滤非语义噪声的机制，提出浅层语义伪装（SSC）策略，将扰动生成限制在语义有效子空间中以绕过预训练权重的语义抑制。

Result: 实验表明SSC方法在浅层冻结和语义聚焦预训练等挑战性范式下仍能保持数据的不可学习性。

Conclusion: 本文填补了基于预训练的不可学习学习的关键空白，为UE在PF范式下的应用提供了系统性理解和方法支持。

Abstract: The unauthorized use of personal data in model training has emerged as a growing privacy threat. Unlearnable examples (UEs) address this issue by embedding imperceptible perturbations into benign examples to obstruct feature learning. However, existing studies mainly evaluate UEs under from-scratch training settings, leaving their behavior under the widely adopted pretraining-finetuning (PF) paradigm largely unexplored. In this work, we provide the first systematic investigation of unlearnable examples across diverse training paradigms. Our analysis reveals that loading and freezing pretrained weights significantly weakens the effectiveness of existing UEs methods. We further explain these findings through semantic filtering: while UEs tend to induce models to overfit non-semantic noise, thereby weakening their semantic extraction capabilities, under the PF paradigm, frozen shallow layers preserve data semantics, effectively filtering out distracting information like unlearnable noise. Guided by these insights, we propose a hierarchical deception strategy, Shallow Semantic Camouflage (SSC), that confines the generation process to a semantically valid subspace, aiming to bypass the semantic suppression introduced by pretrained weights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently preserves data unlearnability even under challenging training paradigms, such as shallow-layer freezing and semantic-focused pretraining (SF-Pretrain), bridging the critical gap in pretrain-based unlearnable learning.

</details>


### [104] [MACS: Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling for Efficient Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05225)
*Bo Li,Chuan Wu,shaolin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MACS是一个无需训练的高效推理框架，通过熵加权负载和动态模态自适应容量机制，解决MoE多模态大模型在专家并行推理中因信息异构性和模态动态性导致的效率瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE多模态大模型在专家并行推理中面临严重的效率瓶颈（尾随者效应），现有基于token计数的负载均衡方法无法解决两个关键挑战：(1) 信息异构性：大量冗余视觉token与语义关键token被同等对待；(2) 模态动态性：不同任务中视觉与文本比例变化导致资源分配不均。

Method: 提出MACS（模态感知容量缩放），包含两个核心机制：(1) 熵加权负载机制：量化视觉token的语义价值，解决信息异构性；(2) 动态模态自适应容量机制：根据输入实时模态组成分配专家资源。

Result: 在多个多模态基准上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: MACS为MoE多模态大模型在专家并行推理中的高效部署提供了一种新颖且鲁棒的无需训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE MLLMs) suffer from a significant efficiency bottleneck during Expert Parallelism (EP) inference due to the straggler effect. This issue is worsened in the multimodal context, as existing token-count-based load balancing methods fail to address two unique challenges: (1) Information Heterogeneity, where numerous redundant visual tokens are treated equally to semantically critical ones, and (2) Modality Dynamics, where varying visual to text ratios across tasks lead to resource misallocation. To address these challenges, we propose MACS (Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling), a training-free inference framework. Specifically, MACS introduces an Entropy-Weighted Load mechanism to quantify the semantic value of visual tokens, addressing information heterogeneity. Additionally, the Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity mechanism allocates expert resources based on the real-time modal composition of the input. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MACS significantly outperforms existing methods on various multimodal benchmarks, providing a novel and robust solution for the efficient deployment of MoE MLLMs in EP inference.

</details>


### [105] [Internalizing Outcome Supervision into Process Supervision: A New Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05226)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Sibo wang,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种监督内化方法，使模型能够在仅有结果监督的条件下，通过识别、纠正和复用失败推理轨迹，自动提取过程级学习信号，实现细粒度策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习推理方法面临结果级奖励难以进行精确信用分配的问题，而外部过程监督成本高昂且难以持续扩展，亟需一种能从结果监督中自动获取过程级反馈信号的方法。

Method: 将推理强化学习理解为将结果监督内化为过程监督的问题，提出监督内化方法：模型在强化学习过程中自动识别、纠正并复用失败的推理轨迹，从中提取过程级学习信号，实现细粒度信用分配。

Result: 使模型在仅有结果监督的条件下实现了过程级的精细化策略优化，开辟了不同于外部过程监督的细粒度信用分配新路径。

Conclusion: 推理强化学习的核心问题可以归结为结果监督的内化问题，所提出的监督内化范式为自动生成和优化内部过程监督信号提供了可行方案，为未来推理任务中的细粒度学习提供了新方向。

Abstract: The central challenge of reinforcement learning for reasoning lies not only in the sparsity of outcome-level supervision, but more fundamentally in how to transform feedback provided only at the end of a sequence into fine-grained learning signals that can guide intermediate reasoning steps. Existing approaches either rely on outcome-level rewards for sequence-level optimization, which makes precise credit assignment difficult, or depend on externally constructed process supervision, which is costly and difficult to scale sustainably. To address this, we propose a new perspective: reinforcement learning for reasoning can be understood as the problem of internalizing outcome supervision into process supervision. From this perspective, we introduce a supervision-internalization method for reinforcement learning for reasoning, enabling the model to automatically extract process-level learning signals through identifying, correcting, and reusing failed reasoning trajectories, thereby achieving finer-grained policy optimization under outcome-only supervision. We further abstract this idea into a new training paradigm, in which the model continually generates and refines its own internal process supervision during reinforcement learning, opening a new path for fine-grained credit assignment in reinforcement learning for reasoning that differs from externally provided process supervision.

</details>


### [106] [Rethinking Data Curation in LLM Training: Online Reweighting Offers Better Generalization than Offline Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05227)
*Wanru Zhao,Yihong Chen,Yuzhi Tang,Wentao Ma,Shengchao Hu,Shell Xu Hu,Alex Iacob,Abhinav Mehrotra,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ADAPT，一种在线动态数据重加权框架，通过自适应每样本学习率替代传统离线数据筛选与混合方法，在指令微调和大规模预训练中均取得更优的跨基准泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据筛选（data selection）和混合（data mixing）方法采用离线范式，与训练过程脱钩，导致:(1) 模型/任务变化时需重新运行整个流程，工程开销大且脆弱；(2) 通过硬过滤或重采样改变数据规模，牺牲了数据多样性，损害泛化能力。

Method: 将数据策重视为在线重加权问题，提出ADAPT框架：(1) 基于相似性的质量信号动态调整每样本的学习率（即样本权重）；(2) 不改变训练样本数量；(3) 隐式实现课程学习，随模型进化从粗粒度模式逐步过渡到细粒度语义区分。

Result: 在指令微调和大规模预训练实验中，ADAPT在同等FLOPs下一致优于离线筛选/混合方法以及已有的在线方法，展现出更强的跨基准泛化能力。

Conclusion: 在线动态重加权是比离线静态数据策展更优的范式，ADAPT通过自适应每样本学习率有效解决了传统方法的多样性损失和工程脆弱性问题。

Abstract: Data curation is a critical yet under-explored area in large language model (LLM) training. Existing methods, such as data selection and mixing, operate in an offline paradigm, detaching themselves from training. This separation introduces engineering overhead and makes the curation brittle: the entire pipeline must be re-run under model/task shifts. Moreover, offline methods alter data size through hard filtering or resampling, often sacrificing data diversity and harming generalization. We propose to rethink data curation as an online reweighting problem, where sample importance is dynamically adjusted during training via loss weighting rather than static pre-processing. Specifically, we introduce ADAPT (Adaptive Data reweighting for Pretraining and FineTuning), a dynamic online framework that reweights training samples with adaptive per-sample learning rates guided by similarity-based quality signals, without changing the number of training samples. Unlike offline methods that enforce a static data distribution, ADAPT acts as an implicit curriculum learner, progressively shifting focus from coarse-grained patterns to fine-grained semantic distinctions as the model evolves. Experiments on both instruction tuning and large-scale pretraining show that ADAPT consistently outperforms offline selection/mixing and prior online methods, achieving stronger cross-benchmark generalization under equal FLOPs.

</details>


### [107] [Attribution-Guided Continual Learning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05285)
*Yazheng Liu,Yuxuan Wan,Rui Xu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于归因引导的持续微调框架，通过估计每个Transformer层中任务特定的参数重要性分数来调控梯度更新，在持续学习中更好地平衡旧任务保持和新任务学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM持续学习方法（数据重放、参数冻结、正则化）缺乏对内部知识分布的语义感知，无法区分哪些参数应该保留或更新，导致灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 提出归因引导的持续微调框架：估计每个Transformer层中任务特定的、元素级的参数重要性分数，利用这些分数调控梯度——对先前任务重要的参数更新幅度小，不重要的参数保持可塑性以学习新任务。

Result: 在持续学习基准测试中，该方法一致优于基线方法，在保持旧任务性能的同时，在新任务上取得有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: 通过感知参数对任务的重要性来指导梯度更新，能有效缓解LLM在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，实现旧任务保持与新任务学习的更好权衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often suffer from catastrophic forgetting in continual learning: after learning new tasks sequentially, they perform worse on earlier tasks. Existing methods mitigate catastrophic forgetting by data replay, parameter freezing, or regularization. However, these methods lack semantic awareness of internal knowledge distribution in LLMs. As a result, they cannot distinguish parameters that should be preserved or updated. We propose an attribution-guided continual fine-tuning framework for LLMs. Our method estimates task-specific, element-wise parameter importance in each Transformer layer and uses these scores to modulate gradients. Parameters important to previous tasks receive smaller updates, while less relevant ones remain plastic for learning new tasks. Experiments on continual learning benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms baselines, achieving better retention of old tasks while maintaining competitive performance on new tasks.

</details>


### [108] [COPYCOP: Ownership Verification for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05360)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CopyCop算法，用于检测是否存在一个GNN被训练来模仿另一个GNN的节点嵌入，即使面对不同的架构、权重、嵌入维度以及嵌入变换，该算法依然有效且具有理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印和指纹方法无法在GNN架构不同、权重不同、嵌入维度不同且存在对抗变换的条件下，识别出模仿其他GNN嵌入的复制品（copycat GNN）。

Method: 提出名为CopyCop的算法，能够在两个GNN输出节点嵌入的情况下，判断它们是否独立训练，还是其中一个刻意模仿另一个的嵌入。

Result: 在14个数据集和5种GNN架构上的实验表明，CopyCop准确且鲁棒，能够抵抗广泛的对抗攻击和嵌入变换。

Conclusion: CopyCop能够在严格条件下（不同架构、权重、嵌入维度、存在嵌入变换）有效识别copycat GNN，优于现有方法，并提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Given two GNNs that output node embeddings, how can we determine if they were trained independently? An adversary could have trained one GNN specifically to mimic the other GNN's embeddings. To obscure this relationship between the GNNs, the adversarial GNN might then transform its output embeddings. The two GNNs could have different architectures, weights, and embedding dimensions, and the adversary can transform the embeddings. Despite these stringent conditions, our algorithm (named CopyCop) can identify such copycat GNNs, unlike existing watermarking and fingerprinting methods. We also provide theoretical guarantees for CopyCop. Finally, experiments on 14 datasets and 5 GNN architectures demonstrate that CopyCop is accurate and robust against a broad class of adversarial attacks and transformations. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CopyCop-Graph-Ownership-Verification-8143/README.md

</details>


### [109] [SPADE: Faster Drug Discovery by Learning from Sparse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05370)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Ben Fauber,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPADE提出了一种新的配体选择算法，仅需平均40次测试就能找到10个高质量配体，在样本效率上比深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法中位数提升7%-32%，且速度比最接近的竞争者快10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现需要找到能与靶标蛋白强结合且具有选择性的配体分子，但候选配体中仅有不到5%能通过早期阶段的筛选。此外，针对缺乏先验数据的新型蛋白质，需要从零开始迭代筛选，目标是尽可能用最少的测试次数找到足够数量的高质量配体。

Method: SPADE通过从稀疏数据中学习的方法进行配体选择，提出了一种新颖的迭代筛选策略，在候选药物评分速度上实现了10倍的提升。

Result: SPADE平均仅需40次测试就能找到10个高质量配体；在逐对比较中，SPADE在更多蛋白质上优于深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法，样本效率中位数提升7%-32%；且评分速度比最接近的竞争者快10倍。

Conclusion: SPADE在样本效率和计算速度上均显著优于现有方法，为新型蛋白质的药物发现提供了一种高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Drug discovery seeks molecules (ligands) that bind strongly and selectively to a target protein. However, fewer than 5% of candidate ligands pass the bar for even the early stages of drug discovery. Furthermore, we want methods that work for novel proteins for which we have no prior data. Starting from scratch, we have to iteratively select and test candidate ligands such that we find enough ligands of the desired quality in as few tests as possible. Our proposed algorithm, named SPADE, introduces a novel approach to ligand selection that requires only 40 tests on average to find 10 high-quality ligands. In one-vs-one comparisons, SPADE outperforms deep learning and Bayesian optimization methods on more proteins, achieving median improvements of 7%-32% in sample efficiency. SPADE is also 10x faster than its closest competitor at scoring candidate drugs. Dataset and code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SPADE_Fast_Drug_Discovery_by_Learning_from_Sparse_Data-F028/README.md

</details>
