Abstract: Group-relative RL training (GRPO) samples a small group of parallel rollouts for every training prompt and uses their within-group reward spread to compute per-trajectory advantages. In agentic environments each rollout is a long multi-turn dialogue with one LLM call per step, so this multi-sample multiplier dominates the total training cost. When every rollout of a prompt ends with the same reward, the group has zero reward variance and contributes no gradient, so the extra rollouts add no information; such groups are common in practice (typically around 40% of all groups), so the wasted-compute fraction is substantial rather than marginal. Existing methods filter such groups at the prompt level, either after their rollouts are paid for or before any rollout begins, but both decide without using information that becomes available during the rollout itself. We instead ask whether the in-group divergence between the partial trajectories at an intermediate step can already predict that the group will be zero-variance: when the parallel rollouts have already converged on the same action prefix, the group is on track to produce a single reward, and we can stop early. We propose a one-parameter gate that stops a group when the mean pairwise prefix edit distance between its partial action sequences falls below a threshold. On a 60-iteration on-policy GRPO run on ALFWorld with Qwen2.5-7B, averaged over four random seeds, the gated arm finishes 10.7% faster in wall-clock (bootstrap 95% CI excludes 0) and shifts held-out success rate on 50 unseen tasks by +2.5 pp, with the held-out gain tracing to a measurable reduction in zero-advantage gradient-batch dilution. Code is available at https://github.com/zhiyuanZhai20/selective-rollout.

</details>


### [147] [Retrieval from Within: An Intrinsic Capability of Attention-Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05806)
*Elad Hoffer,Yochai Blau,Ron Banner,Daniel Soudry,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出INTRA框架，通过注意力机制让编码器-解码器模型直接从内部表征中检索证据，统一了检索与生成过程，在QA任务上优于传统RAG流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG将检索和生成视为分离的系统，存在检索器与生成器不匹配的问题，且需要外部检索模块。本文探索是否可以利用注意力模型自身的内部表征直接实现检索。

Method: INTRA框架中，解码器的注意力查询对预编码的证据块进行评分，这些证据块直接被复用为生成的上下文。通过设计，检索和生成被统一，编码器状态可在查询间复用。

Result: 在问答基准上，INTRA在证据召回率和端到端答案质量上均优于强工程化的检索流水线。

Conclusion: 基于注意力的模型已经具备可被激发而非作为外部模块添加的检索机制。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) typically treats retrieval and generation as separate systems. We ask whether an attention-based encoder-decoder can instead retrieve directly from its own internal representations. We introduce INTRA (INTrinsic Retrieval via Attention), a framework where decoder attention queries score pre-encoded evidence chunks that are then directly reused as context for generation. By construction, INTRA unifies retrieval and generation, eliminating the retriever-generator mismatch typical of RAG pipelines. This design also amortizes context encoding by reusing precomputed encoder states across queries. On question-answering benchmarks, INTRA outperforms strong engineered retrieval pipelines on both evidence recall and end-to-end answer quality. Our results demonstrate that attention-based models already possess a retrieval mechanism that can be elicited, rather than added as an external module.

</details>


### [148] [A Testable Certificate for Constant Collapse in Teacher-Guided VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05813)
*Zegu Zhang,Jianhua Peng,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可量化的后验坍缩判定边界：当潜在变量仅依赖输入的变分编码器（VAE）的原始证据下界（ELBO）损失低于恒定学生（仅依赖数据集平均的教师分布）的损失时，可以严格证明该模型未发生“输入无关的常数坍缩”。该边界通过教师互信息 \(I_T(X;T)\) 计算，并在CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet-200实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过KL项小、解码器强、潜在编码使用弱等间接症状诊断后验坍缩，但缺乏明确的坍缩边界。本文旨在为“输入无关的常数坍缩”这一具体失效模式给出可测量的判定阈值。

Method: 1. 理论推导：对于任何非恒定的教师分布 \(T(\cdot\mid x)\)，最佳常数学生是数据集平均教师分布，其对齐损失为教师互信息 \(I_T(X;T)\)。2. 构建“原始见证”（raw witness）损失，若其低于 \(I_T(X;T)\) 且有一定安全裕度，则模型不能是输入无关的常数。3. 在CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet-200上进行实验验证。

Result: 1. 在CIFAR-100中，全训练保持在认证边界的安全侧；去除对齐使原始见证落入常数学生区间；从坍缩检查点重启并启用对齐可恢复认证。2. Tiny-ImageNet-200中，三个独立搜索的教师均呈现相同的“预防—坍缩—恢复”模式。3. 标准VAE基线（包括保持重建质量或事后可预测性的方法）在原始认证下均为负。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种窄但严格的认证：它保证匹配的非恒定教师相关的变异性通过了潜在路径，而非排除所有后验坍缩形式。

Abstract: Posterior collapse in variational autoencoders is often diagnosed by its symptoms: a small KL term, a strong decoder, or weak use of the latent code. These signals are useful, but they do not define a collapse boundary. We study a concrete failure mode, input-independent constant collapse, and show that this case admits an exact threshold. For any fixed nonconstant teacher distribution \(T(\cdot\mid x)\), the best constant student is the dataset-average teacher distribution, and its alignment cost is the teacher mutual information \(I_T(X;T)\). Therefore, if a strictly latent-only raw witness achieves alignment loss below this value, with a safety margin, the witness cannot be constant in the input.
  This identity turns a qualitative failure mode into a measurable one. In CIFAR-100 experiments with per-seed teacher search, full training stays on the certified side of the boundary, removing alignment drives the raw witness into the constant-student regime, and restarting from a collapsed checkpoint with alignment enabled restores the certificate. Tiny-ImageNet-200 fixed-target runs show the same prevention--collapse--rescue pattern across three independently searched teachers. Standard VAE-style baselines, including methods that preserve reconstruction quality or post-hoc predictability, remain negative under the raw certificate. The guarantee is intentionally narrow: it certifies that the matched nonconstant teacher-relative variation passes through the latent pathway, rather than claiming that all forms of posterior collapse have been ruled out.

</details>


### [149] [HCInfer: An Efficient Inference System via Error Compensation for Resource-Constrained Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05819)
*Shen Xu,Xiangwen Zhuge,Zhe Xu,Yingkun Hu,Zheng Yang,Yunhao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HCInfer是一个异构推理系统，将残差补偿卸载到CPU，压缩主干在GPU上执行，通过异步补偿流水线和敏感度感知的动态秩分配，在内存受限的消费级硬件上实现高效的大模型推理，相比压缩模型准确率提升最高5.2%，相比全精度模型加速最高10.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型参数量巨大，在消费级硬件上部署受内存限制。现有模型压缩和卸载方法存在精度显著下降或严重吞吐瓶颈的问题。

Method: 提出HCInfer异构推理系统，将补偿参数（LoRA风格分支）卸载到CPU，压缩主干模型保留在GPU，并引入异步补偿流水线来隐藏补偿开销，以及敏感度感知的动态秩分配来最大化精度恢复。

Result: 在下游任务上相比压缩模型取得最高5.2%的准确率提升，相比全精度模型取得最高10.4倍的加速。

Conclusion: HCInfer通过异构计算和补偿机制，有效解决了大模型在消费级硬件上部署的内存限制与性能瓶颈问题，兼顾了精度与效率。

Abstract: LLMs often struggle with memory-constrained deployment on consumer-grade hardware due to their massive parameter sizes. While existing solutions such as model compression and offloading improve deployment feasibility, they often suffer from substantial accuracy degradation or severe throughput bottlenecks. Recent error compensation methods recover accuracy through auxiliary LoRA-style branches, and we observe that these branches are inherently amenable to offloading: they require substantial parameter storage but access only a small subset of compensation parameters during each inference step. Motivated by this opportunity, we propose HCInfer, a heterogeneous inference system that offloads residual compensation to the CPU while executing the compressed backbone on the GPU, and further introduces an asynchronous compensation pipeline and sensitivity-aware dynamic rank allocation to hide compensation overhead and maximize accuracy recovery. Experimental results show that HCInfer achieves a maximum accuracy improvement of 5.2% on downstream tasks compared to compression model and sustaining a maximum speedup of 10.4x compared to full-precision model.

</details>


### [150] [MDN: Parallelizing Stepwise Momentum for Delta Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05838)
*Yulong Huang,Xiang Liu,Hongxiang Huang,Xiaopeng Lin,Zunchang Liu,Xiaowen Chu,Zeke Xie,Bojun Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动量DeltaNet (MDN)，通过几何重排更新系数实现动量级联法则的分块并行算法，并从动力系统角度设计稳定门控约束，使线性注意力模型在保持训练效率的同时显著提升长序列建模性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性注意力模型（如Mamba2、GDN）将线性递归解释为在线SGD，但朴素SGD更新存在信息快速衰减和优化收敛次优的问题。动量优化器虽有潜力，但在训练效率和有效性之间难以兼得。

Method: 1) 提出分块并行算法，通过几何重排更新系数实现步进式动量规则；2) 从动力系统视角分析动量基递归为二阶系统，引入复共轭特征值，据此设计稳定门控约束；3) 利用Triton内核实现高效训练。

Result: 在400M和1.3B参数模型上，MDN在多项下游评估基准中持续优于Transformer、Mamba2和GDN等强基线，且训练吞吐量与Mamba2、KDA等竞争模型相当。

Conclusion: 本文提出的MDN有效解决了线性注意力中SGD更新的信息衰减问题，通过动量机制和理论指导的门控设计，在长序列LLM扩展中实现了效率与性能的双赢。

Abstract: Linear Attention (LA) offers a promising paradigm for scaling large language models (LLMs) to long sequences by avoiding the quadratic complexity of self-attention. Recent LA models such as Mamba2 and GDN interpret linear recurrences as closed-form online stochastic gradient descent (SGD), but naive SGD updates suffer from rapid information decay and suboptimal convergence in optimization. While momentum-based optimizers provide a natural remedy, they pose challenges in simultaneously achieving training efficiency and effectiveness. To address this, we develop a chunkwise parallel algorithm for LA with a stepwise momentum rule by geometrically reordering the update coefficients. Further, from a dynamical systems perspective, we analyze the momentum-based recurrence as a second-order system that introduces complex conjugate eigenvalues. This analysis guides the design of stable gating constraints. The resulting model, Momentum DeltaNet (MDN), leverages Triton kernels to achieve comparable training throughput with competitive linear models such as Mamba2 and KDA. Extensive experiments on the 400M and 1.3B parameter models demonstrate consistent performance improvements over strong baselines, including Transformers, Mamba2 and GDN, across diverse downstream evaluation benchmarks. Code: https://github.com/HuuYuLong/MomentumDeltaNet .

</details>


### [151] [Hypothesis generation and updating in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05851)
*Hua-Dong Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过数字游戏研究了LLM在推理中的假设生成与更新能力。LLM的行为可以用带偏见的贝叶斯模型描述：默认情况下存在强采样假设（隐含奥卡姆剃刀，偏好窄假设），思考模式则更依赖先验。存在评估-生成差距：评估时更准确，生成时更简单规则化。这种模式无法外推——模型对观测数据表现良好，但对未覆盖的假设域泛化差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在部分信息下生成假设并随新信息更新的推理能力，以及这种推理与最优贝叶斯模型的接近程度。

Method: 通过数字游戏设置控制实验，使用三种互补探针（后验预测、假设评估、假设生成）测量假设后验，并将LLM行为与最优贝叶斯模型和人类行为对比。

Result: LLM行为可用两参数贝叶斯拟合描述，但存在系统性偏差：默认强采样假设隐含奥卡姆剃刀偏好窄假设，思考模式转向依赖先验；存在评估-生成差距；该模式无法外推到未覆盖的假设域。

Conclusion: LLM作为通用问题求解器存在局限性，特别是在需要假设超越数据的科学推理中表现不佳。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly help people solve problems, from debugging code to repairing machinery. This process requires generating plausible hypotheses from partial descriptions, then updating them as more information arrives. Yet how LLMs perform this form of inference, and how close it is to optimal, remains unclear. We study this question in the number game, a controlled setting in which a learner infers the hypothesis supported by a few positive integers, such as $\{16, 8, 2, 64\}$: a rule like powers of 2 or an interval like numbers near 20. We measure the posterior over hypotheses using three complementary probes: posterior prediction, hypothesis evaluation, and hypothesis generation. We then compare LLM behavior with an optimal Bayesian model and human behavior, and test whether the same posterior is expressed across probes. LLMs are often well described by a two-parameter Bayesian fit, but with systematic offsets: by default they show a strong-sampling assumption that creates an implicit Occam's razor, favoring narrower hypotheses, while thinking mode shifts them toward greater prior reliance. We also find a robust evaluation--generation gap: LLMs select more correct hypotheses during hypothesis evaluation but generate simpler, more rule-like hypotheses. Finally, this Bayesian-with-bias pattern does not extrapolate. Models can behave as if they hold rule-like hypotheses over observed examples, yet generalize poorly to parts of the hypothesis domain not covered by those examples. Our results highlight a limitation of LLMs as general problem solvers, especially for scientific inference, where hypotheses must go beyond the data.

</details>


### [152] [Measuring Learning Progress via Gradient-Momentum Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05856)
*Samuel Blad,Martin Längkvist,Amy Loutfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出梯度-动量耦合（GMC）信号，通过测量每个样本梯度与历史梯度动量的逐参数归一化绝对乘积，量化样本对持续学习的贡献，用于替代预测误差作为好奇心驱动探索中的学习进度信号，在噪声鲁棒性和课程学习涌现方面表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统好奇心驱动探索中广泛使用的预测误差信号无法有效区分有意义的可学习模式与随机噪声，导致智能体被噪声干扰或无法聚焦于真正可学习的内容。本文旨在设计一种能够衡量样本对持续学习实际贡献的进度信号，以提升探索效率与噪声鲁棒性。

Method: 提出GMC信号，计算每个样本的梯度与动量（历史梯度累积）的逐参数归一化绝对乘积。动量天然具备滤波噪声和振荡的特性，GMC借此识别哪些样本的梯度对当前参数更新有实质性贡献，从而量化学习进度。

Result: 在受控实验中，GMC表现出噪声鲁棒性和涌现课程学习能力，即按任务的学习速度（而非难度）自动排序优先级。在MiniGrid环境中，将现有好奇心驱动架构中的预测误差替换为GMC后，提升了智能体对观测噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GMC是一种更有效的学习进度信号，能够替代预测误差用于好奇心驱动探索，尤其在有噪声干扰的环境中，能够帮助智能体聚焦于真正可学习的内容，提升探索效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Measuring learning progress is essential for curiosity-driven exploration in reinforcement learning, but widely used signals such as prediction error often fail to distinguish meaningful, learnable patterns from random noise. This paper proposes Gradient-Momentum Coupling (GMC), a signal derived from optimization dynamics that quantifies how useful each sample's gradient is for ongoing learning by measuring its per-parameter normalized absolute product with the momentum from previous gradients. By leveraging momentum's natural filtering of noise and oscillations, GMC identifies samples that contribute to ongoing parameter updates. Controlled experiments demonstrate noise robustness and emergent curriculum learning, with the signal prioritizing tasks by learning speed rather than difficulty. Experiments on MiniGrid suggest that replacing prediction error with GMC within existing curiosity-driven architectures can improve robustness to observation noise.

</details>


### [153] [Offline Reinforcement Learning for Rotation Profile Control in Tokamaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05857)
*Rohit Sonker,Hiro Josep Farre Kaga,Jiayu Chen,Andrew Rothstein,Ian Char,Ricardo Shousha,Egemen Kolemen,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究使用离线强化学习（offline RL）和离线基于模型的强化学习算法，仅基于DIII-D托卡马克的历史数据训练等离子体旋转剖面控制策略，并通过概率动力学模型生成rollouts进行训练，最终在实际DIII-D装置上部署并取得了有前景的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托卡马克中控制等离子体旋转剖面对于稳定性、约束和输运至关重要，但由于高维度、多执行器响应以及对等离子体条件的依赖性，全剖面控制极具挑战性。传统方法难以有效解决这一多输入多输出控制问题。

Method: 采用离线RL和离线基于模型的RL算法，仅利用DIII-D托卡马克的历史数据进行训练；最终方法使用等离子体动力学的概率模型生成rollouts用于RL训练，从而在缺乏精确仿真器的情况下学习控制策略。

Result: 将训练的RL策略部署在DIII-D托卡马克上，观察到了有前景的实际控制效果，验证了方法在真实复杂物理设备上的可行性。

Conclusion: 总结了在仅使用有限历史数据的情况下，在复杂物理设备上训练和部署RL策略的关键挑战与洞见，表明离线RL方法为托卡马克旋转剖面控制提供了一种有潜力的解决方案。

Abstract: Tokamaks remain leading candidates for achieving practical fusion energy, yet many important control problems inside these devices are still difficult or unsolved. One such challenge is controlling the plasma rotation profile, which strongly influences stability, confinement, and transport. While the average rotation can be controlled, controlling the full profile is challenging due to high dimensionality, response to multiple actuators and dependence on plasma condition. Learning-based control methods, such as reinforcement learning (RL), provide a potential solution to this challenging problem with ability to model complex interactions leading to effective multi-input multi-output control. However, learning such policies is challenging due to the lack of accurate simulators that can model the rotation profile dynamics. In this work, we investigate the use of offline RL and offline model-based RL algorithms for rotation profile control, training them solely on historical data from the DIII-D tokamak. Our final method uses probabilistic models of plasma dynamics to generate rollouts for RL training. We deploy this policy on the DIII-D Tokamak and observe promising real-world results. We conclude by highlighting key challenges and insights from training and deploying an RL policy on a complex physical device while using only limited past data.

</details>


### [154] [Do Neural Operators Forget Geometry? The Forgetting Hypothesis in Deep Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05862)
*Yanming Xia,Angelica I. Aviles-Rivero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了神经算子在深度增加时系统性地丢失域几何信息的“几何遗忘”现象，并提出了一种轻量级的几何记忆注入机制来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子在非规则几何域上的行为尚未被充分理解，现有研究多聚焦于编码问题，而作者发现这实则是深层算子架构中与深度相关的根本性失效模式。

Method: 形式化提出"几何遗忘假说"，通过逐层几何探测实验验证谱方法和注意力机制算子均存在几何保真度损失；提出轻量级几何记忆注入机制，在中间层恢复几何约束。

Result: 几何遗忘会降低算子精度、稳定性和泛化能力；几何记忆注入可有效缓解遗忘；在Transformer类算子中发现了几何捷径不稳定性。

Conclusion: 几何保持是神经算子架构的结构性要求而非设计选项，该工作为构建几何感知的深度算子提供了理论依据和实用工具。

Abstract: Neural operators perform well on structured domains, yet their behaviour on irregular geometries remains poorly understood. We show that this limitation is not merely an encoding issue, but a depth-wise failure mode inherent to deep operator architectures.
  We formalise the Geometric Forgetting Hypothesis: due to the Markovian structure of operator layers and their reliance on global mixing mechanisms, neural operators progressively lose access to domain geometry as depth increases. Using layer-wise geometric probing, we demonstrate that both spectral and attention-based operators systematically lose geometric fidelity.
  We show that this geometric forgetting degrades accuracy, stability, and generalisation. To counteract it, we introduce a lightweight geometry memory injection mechanism that restores geometric constraints at intermediate depths with minimal architectural overhead. This simple intervention consistently mitigates forgetting and exposes a geometric shortcut instability in transformer-based operators, revealing that geometric retention is a structural requirement rather than a design choice.

</details>


### [155] [SOPE: Stabilizing Off-Policy Evaluation for Online RL with Prior Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05863)
*Carlo Romeo,Girolamo Macaluso,Alessandro Sestini,Andrew D. Bagdanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SOPE算法，利用离线策略评估信号作为早停机制，动态控制离线训练阶段长度，在25个连续控制任务上实现最高45.6%的性能提升和22倍计算开销降低，平衡了样本效率与计算效率的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将先验数据融入在线强化学习虽能加速训练，但面临高计算成本与多阶段训练管线的权衡；固定长度稳定阶段虽计算高效但需任务相关的手动调参，存在浪费先验知识或严重过拟合的风险。

Method: 提出SOPE算法，使用与actor对齐的离线策略评估信号作为自动化早停机制，通过在保留验证集上评估当前策略动作分布下的critic，当分布外收益饱和时精确停止梯度更新，无需手动调度调参。

Result: 在Minari基准套件的25个连续控制任务上，SOPE将基线性能提升最高45.6%，同时将所需TFLOPs降低最高22倍。

Conclusion: 自适应、评估驱动的更新调度比依赖静态、穷举的更新调度更有效，能够更好地平衡样本效率与计算效率之间的权衡。

Abstract: Incorporating prior data into online reinforcement learning accelerates training but typically forces a difficult trade-off between high computational costs and long, multi-stage training pipelines. While fixed-length stabilization phases are significantly more computationally efficient than static update schedules, they require task-dependent manual tuning, risking either the waste of prior knowledge or severe overfitting. To address this, we propose SOPE, an algorithm that uses an actor-aligned Off-Policy Policy Evaluation (OPE) signal as an automated early-stopping mechanism to dynamically control the length of offline training phases. By evaluating the critic on a held-out validation split under the current policy's action distribution, SOPE halts gradient updates exactly when out-of-distribution benefits saturate, eliminating the need for manual schedule tuning. Evaluated on 25 continuous control tasks from the Minari benchmark suite, SOPE improves baseline performance by up to 45.6% while reducing the required TFLOPs by up to 22x, thus balancing the tradeoff between sample and computational efficiency. These findings demonstrate that adaptive, evaluation-driven update schedules are more effective than relying on static, exhaustive update schedules.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Understanding Annotator Safety Policy with Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05329)
*Alex Oesterling,Donghao Ren,Yannick Assogba,Dominik Moritz,Sunnie S. Y. Kim,Leon Gatys,Fred Hohman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可解释的标注者策略模型（APMs），通过标注行为推断标注者内部安全策略，无需额外标注成本即可区分标注分歧的不同来源（操作失误、政策模糊、价值多元），并在LLM和人类标注中验证了其揭示政策模糊和价值差异的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI安全策略中标注分歧普遍存在，其来源包括操作失误、政策模糊和价值多元，区分这些来源对改进标注质量和策略设计至关重要，但直接询问标注者推理过程成本高且不可靠。

Method: 提出Annotator Policy Models (APMs)，通过仅从标注行为中学习可解释的标注者内部安全策略模型，无需额外标注工作；在多种设置下验证模型准确率、反事实预测能力和已知策略差异恢复能力。

Result: APMs准确率超过80%，能忠实预测反事实编辑下的响应，并恢复受控设定中的已知策略差异；成功应用于LLM和人类标注数据，揭示安全指令理解分歧（政策模糊）和不同人口群体的安全优先级差异（价值多元）。

Conclusion: APMs支持更有针对性的、透明的和包容性的安全策略设计，为区分标注分歧来源提供了高效、可扩展的方法。

Abstract: Safety policies define what constitutes safe and unsafe AI outputs, guiding data annotation and model development. However, annotation disagreement is pervasive and can stem from multiple sources such as operational failures (annotators misunderstand or misexecute the task), policy ambiguity (policy wording leaves room for interpretation), or value pluralism (different annotators hold different perspectives on safety). Distinguishing these sources matters. For example, operational failures call for quality control, ambiguity calls for policy clarification, and pluralism calls for deliberation about incorporating diverse perspectives. Yet understanding why annotators disagree is difficult. Directly asking annotators for their reasoning is costly, substantially increasing annotation burden, and can be unreliable for both human and LLM annotators as self-reported reasoning often fails to reflect actual decision processes.
  We introduce Annotator Policy Models (APMs), interpretable models that learn annotators' internal safety policies from labeling behavior alone, making annotator reasoning visible and comparable without additional annotation effort. We validate that APMs accurately model annotator safety policy (>80% accuracy), faithfully predict responses to counterfactual edits, and recover known policy differences in controlled settings. Applying APMs to LLM and human annotations, we demonstrate two core applications: (1) surfacing policy ambiguity by revealing how annotators interpret safety instructions differently, and (2) surfacing value pluralism by uncovering systematic differences in safety priorities across demographic groups. Together, these capabilities support more targeted, transparent, and inclusive safety policy design.

</details>


### [157] [Resolving the bias-precision paradox with stochastic causal representation learning for personalized medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05706)
*Peisong Zhang,Manqiang Peng,Yuxuan Wu,Pawit Phadungsaksawasdi,Wesley Yeung,Ye Zhang,Trang Nguyen,Qiang Zhang,Nan Liu,Meng Wang,Kee Yuan Ngiam,Yih-Chung Tham,Ching-Yu Cheng,Tianfan Fu,Qingyu Chen,Rosemary Ke,Chang Li,Wenzhuo Yang,Zhenghao Lu,Chunyou Lai,Yu Zhang,Sheng Zhong,Hao Deng,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于采样的最大均值差异（sMMD）方法，通过子集级匹配替代全局对抗性平衡，解决因果表征学习中偏差-精度悖论，在大型ICU队列中实现更准确的个体化治疗效果估计与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在减少混杂偏差时往往会抑制临床上有信息量的异质性，导致患者特异性预测变差，存在偏差-精度悖论。

Method: 提出采样最大均值差异（sMMD）随机对齐策略，用子集级匹配替代全局对抗性平衡，并在反事实结果预测框架中结合归因可解释性。

Result: 在两大ICU队列（n=27,783）上，分布偏移下误差降低11.5%，高风险任务召回率显著提升；人机协作实验中使临床医生准确率提升14.7%，决策时间缩短。

Conclusion: sMMD通过选择性保留临床决定性变量，实现了可解释、实时的临床决策支持，优于实习医生和大语言模型。

Abstract: Estimating individualized treatment effects from longitudinal observational data is central to data-driven medicine, yet existing methods face a fundamental limitation: reducing confounding bias often suppresses clinically informative heterogeneity, degrading patient-specific predictions. Here, we identify this tension as a bias-precision paradox in causal representation learning and introduce sampling-based maximum mean discrepancy (sMMD), a stochastic alignment strategy that replaces global adversarial balancing with subset-level matching. We instantiate this approach in a framework for counterfactual outcome prediction with attribution-grounded interpretability. Across two large-scale ICU cohorts (n = 27,783), our framework improves accuracy under distribution shift, reducing error by up to 11.5% and substantially increasing recall in high-risk tasks. Mechanistic analyses show that sMMD selectively preserves clinically decisive variables. In human-AI evaluation, our method outperforms clinicians-in-training and large language models, and improves clinician accuracy by 14.7% while reducing decision time, enabling interpretable, real-time clinical decision support.

</details>


### [158] [ZAYA1-8B Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05365)
*Robert Washbourne,Rishi Iyer,Tomas Figliolia,Henry Zheng,Ryan Lorig-Roach,Sungyeon Yang,Pritish Yuvraj,Quentin Anthony,Yury Tokpanov,Xiao Yang,Ganesh Nanduru,Stephen Ebert,Praneeth Medepalli,Skyler Szot,Srivatsan Rajagopal,Alex Ong,Bhavana Mehta,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZAYA1-8B是一个基于MoE++架构的推理专注模型，仅700M活跃参数（共8B），在AMD全栈平台上训练。它在数学和编程基准上匹配或超越DeepSeek-R1-0528，并通过四阶段RL级联和Markovian RSA测试时计算方法，在AIME'25上达到91.9%，接近更大模型如Gemini-2.5 Pro。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明一个参数不到1B活跃参数的MoE模型，通过从预训练就引入推理数据、精心设计的RL级联训练策略以及高效的测试时计算方法，能够在数学和编程等推理密集型任务上匹敌甚至超越远大于自己的开源推理模型，并缩小与更大规模闭源模型的差距。

Method: 1) 采用MoE++架构，700M活跃/8B总参数；2) 全栈AMD平台训练；3) 从预训练阶段开始引入推理数据，使用答案保留修剪方案；4) 四阶段RL级联：推理热身→400任务RLVE-Gym课程→数学/代码RL（含测试时计算轨迹和合成代码环境）→行为RL；5) 提出Markovian RSA测试时计算方法，递归聚合并行推理轨迹，仅携带有限长度的推理尾。

Result: 在多个数学和编程基准上匹配或超越DeepSeek-R1-0528；在测试时计算评估中，Markovian RSA使ZAYA1-8B在AIME'25上达91.9%、HMMT'25上达89.6%，仅需4K token的推理尾，缩小了与Gemini-2.5 Pro、DeepSeek-V3.2、GPT-5-High等更大模型的差距。

Conclusion: ZAYA1-8B证明小参数推理模型的巨大潜力，高效的训练策略和测试时计算方法能显著提升推理能力，展现出与大规模模型竞争的能力。

Abstract: We present ZAYA1-8B, a reasoning-focused mixture-of-experts (MoE) model with 700M active and 8B total parameters, built on Zyphra's MoE++ architecture. ZAYA1-8B's core pretraining, midtraining, and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) were performed on a full-stack AMD compute, networking, and software platform. With under 1B active parameters, ZAYA1-8B matches or exceeds DeepSeek-R1-0528 on several challenging mathematics and coding benchmarks, and remains competitive with substantially larger open-weight reasoning models. ZAYA1-8B was trained from scratch for reasoning, with reasoning data included from pretraining onward using an answer-preserving trimming scheme. Post-training uses a four-stage RL cascade: reasoning warmup on math and puzzles; a 400-task RLVE-Gym curriculum; math and code RL with test-time compute traces and synthetic code environments built from competitive-programming references; and behavioral RL for chat and instruction following. We also introduce Markovian RSA, a test-time compute method that recursively aggregates parallel reasoning traces while carrying forward only bounded-length reasoning tails between rounds. In TTC evaluation, Markovian RSA raises ZAYA1-8B to 91.9\% on AIME'25 and 89.6\% on HMMT'25 while carrying forward only a 4K-token tail, narrowing the gap to much larger reasoning models including Gemini-2.5 Pro, DeepSeek-V3.2, and GPT-5-High.

</details>


### [159] [BioResearcher: Scenario-Guided Multi-Agent for Translational Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05985)
*Remigiusz Kinas,Joanna Krawczyk,Rafał Powalski,Przemysław Pietrzak,Agnieszka Kowalewska,Krzysztof Kolmus,Maciej Sypetkowski,Łukasz Smoliński,Tomasz Jetka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ingenix BioResearcher，一个场景引导的多智能体系统，用于转化医学中的证据合成。该系统将查询映射到版本化研究手册，通过30+工具和ML端点委派给专门子智能体，结合结构化数据库访问和沙盒代码进行基因组规模分析，并在编辑整合前进行跨模型声明级协调。在单元级能力、开放式推理和端到端临床发现评估中均取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转化医学需要将未明确的开发目标转化为结合文献、试验、专利和定量多组学分析的证据合成，同时保留标识符、不确定性和可追溯来源。通用基础模型和现成工具增强或多智能体系统不适用于此，它们倾向于产生单次回答或无限制运行，无法满足异构生物医学来源所需的可审计、场景特定工作流。

Method: 提出Ingenix BioResearcher多智能体系统：将查询映射到版本化研究手册，委派给30+工具和ML端点的专门子智能体，混合结构化数据库访问与沙盒代码进行基因组规模分析，应用声明级多模型协调后再进行编辑整合。

Result: 在109个单步测试中达到83.49%通过率（平均分0.892）；BixBench-Verified-50上89.33%；BaisBench科学发现平均分0.758；30个临床端到端查询中正向命中率74.7%±3.3%，负向清除率96.8%±0.2%。

Conclusion: Ingenix BioResearcher在单元级、开放式和端到端临床评估中展现出广泛且具有竞争力的性能，有效解决了现有通用模型和系统在转化医学证据合成中的不足。

Abstract: Translational medicine turns underspecified development goals into evidence synthesis that must combine literature, trials, patents, and quantitative multi-omics analysis while preserving identifiers, uncertainty, and retrievable provenance. General-purpose foundation models and off-the-shelf tool-augmented or multi-agent systems are not built for this: they tend to produce single-shot answers or run open-endedly, and fall short on the auditable, scenario-specific workflows that heterogeneous biomedical sources demand.
  This paper introduces Ingenix BioResearcher, a scenario-guided multi-agent system that maps queries to versioned research playbooks, delegates to specialized subagents over 30+ tools and machine-learning endpoints, mixes structured database access with sandboxed code for genome-scale analyses, and applies claim-level multi-model reconciliation before editorial assembly.
  We evaluate BioResearcher across unit-level capabilities, open-ended biomedical reasoning, and end-to-end clinical discovery. It leads evaluated baselines on 109 single-step tests (83.49% pass rate; 0.892 average score), achieves strong biomedical benchmark performance (89.33% on BixBench-Verified-50 and the top 0.758 mean score on BaisBench Scientific Discovery), and leads on a 30-query clinical end-to-end benchmark with the highest positive hit rate (74.7% $\pm$ 3.3%) and negative clear rate (96.8% $\pm$ 0.2%). These results show broad, competitive performance across unit-level, open-ended, and end-to-end clinical evaluations.

</details>


### [160] [Partial Evidence Bench: Benchmarking Authorization-Limited Evidence in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05379)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Partial Evidence Bench，一个用于衡量企业智能体在受限检索环境中因访问控制导致证据缺失但仍输出看似完整答案这一故障模式的确定性基准测试，包含72个任务和三种场景族，从正确性、完整性感知、缺口报告质量和危险完整性行为四个维度评估系统表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业智能体在受作用域限制的检索系统、委托工作流和策略约束的证据环境中运行时，访问控制可能被正确执行，但系统仍输出看似完整的答案，而关键证据实际上位于调用者的授权边界之外。这种"沉默过滤"故障模式在治理层面极为危险，但此前缺乏可量化的测评手段。

Method: 构建了包含尽职调查、合规审计和安全事件响应三种场景族共72个任务的确定性基准测试，采用ACL分区的语料库，提供标准答案（完整视图/授权视图）、完整性判断和结构化缺口报告。从答案正确性、完整性感知、缺口报告质量和危险完整性行为四个维度评估系统。

Result: 基线测试表明沉默过滤在所有场景族中均具有灾难性的不安全性；显式的"失败并报告"行为能消除危险完整性而不导致任务退化到简单的弃权。初步真实模型运行显示，在是否过度宣称完整性、保守地低估完整性或以企业可用形式报告不完整性方面存在模型依赖和场景敏感性差异。

Conclusion: 该基准测试的贡献在于使治理关键的智能体故障可被量化测量，无需人工评判或易受污染的静态语料库。

Abstract: Enterprise agents increasingly operate inside scoped retrieval systems, delegated workflows, and policy-constrained evidence environments. In these settings, access control can be enforced correctly while the system still produces an answer that appears complete even though material evidence lies outside the caller's authorization boundary. This paper introduces Partial Evidence Bench, a deterministic benchmark for measuring that failure mode. The benchmark ships three scenario families -- due diligence, compliance audit, and security incident response -- with 72 tasks total, ACL-partitioned corpora, oracle complete answers, oracle authorized-view answers, oracle completeness judgments, and structured gap-report oracles. It evaluates systems along four surfaces: answer correctness, completeness awareness, gap-report quality, and unsafe completeness behavior. Checked-in baselines show that silent filtering is catastrophically unsafe across all shipped families, while explicit fail-and-report behavior eliminates unsafe completeness without collapsing the task into trivial abstention. Preliminary real-model runs show model-dependent and scenario-sensitive differences in whether systems overclaim completeness, conservatively underclaim, or report incompleteness in an enterprise-usable form. The benchmark's broader contribution is to make a governance-critical agent failure measurable without human judges or contamination-prone static corpora.

</details>


### [161] [BALAR : A Bayesian Agentic Loop for Active Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05386)
*Aymen Echarghaoui,Dongxia Wu,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BALAR是一种无需微调的任务无关外循环算法，通过维护结构化信念、最大化互信息选择澄清问题、动态扩展状态表示，在三个多轮交互基准（AR-Bench-DC、AR-Bench-SP、iCraft-MD）上显著优于所有基线，准确率分别提升14.6%、38.5%和30.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在交互式多轮对话场景中缺乏主动推理机制，大多被动响应用户，无法系统性地推理缺失信息并决定下一个应问的问题。

Method: BALAR采用贝叶斯主动推理循环：维护隐状态的结构化信念分布，通过最大化期望互信息（E-MI）选择最具信息量的澄清问题，当当前状态表示不足以建模时动态扩展状态空间，整个过程无需微调。

Result: 在AR-Bench-DC（侦探推理）上准确率提升14.6%，AR-Bench-SP（思维谜题）提升38.5%，iCraft-MD（临床诊断）提升30.5%，均显著超越所有基线方法。

Conclusion: 主动信息获取和结构化信念维护能有效提升LLM在多轮交互任务中的表现，且BALAR作为任务无关的外循环算法具有良好的泛化性和实用性。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly operate in interactive settings where solving a task requires multiple rounds of information exchange with a user. However, most current systems treat dialogue reactively and lack a principled mechanism to reason about what information is missing and which question should be asked next. We propose BALAR (Bayesian Agentic Loop for Active Reasoning), a task-agnostic outer-loop algorithm that requires no fine-tuning and enables structured multi-turn interaction between an LLM agent and a user. BALAR maintains a structured belief over latent states, selects clarifying questions by maximizing expected mutual information, and dynamically expands its state representation when the current one proves insufficient. We evaluate BALAR on three diverse benchmarks: AR-Bench-DC (detective cases), AR-Bench-SP (thinking puzzles), and iCraft-MD (clinical diagnosis). BALAR significantly outperforms all baselines across all three benchmarks, with $14.6\%$ higher accuracy on AR-Bench-DC, $38.5\%$ on AR-Bench-SP, and $30.5\%$ on iCraft-MD.

</details>


### [162] [Intelligent CCTV for Urban Design: AI-Based Analysis of Soft Infrastructure at Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05402)
*Vinit Katariya,Seungjin Kim,Curtis Craig,Nichole Morris,Hamed Tabkhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于AI的 analytics 框架，利用现有 CCTV 数据评估软性干预措施（临时行人避难岛、路缘扩展）对车速和安全的影响。在明尼阿波利斯的研究显示，无信号交叉口的平均车速和85%分位车速分别下降18.75%和16.56%，通过交通量减少12.2%；信号交叉口平均车速下降20.0%，85%分位车速下降17.19%。证明软基础设施的交通稳静化效果及AI方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通干预评估方法成本高、周期长，缺乏利用现有基础设施（如CCTV）进行快速、低成本评估的手段。本文旨在探索AI和计算机视觉技术能否有效利用已有CCTV数据来评估软性干预措施（临时行人避难岛、路缘扩展）对驾驶行为（车速、安全性）的影响，为交通政策提供证据支持。

Method: 采用AI驱动的 analytics 框架，结合深度学习（用于车辆检测与跟踪）和基于透视变换的速度估计算法，利用现有CCTV摄像头采集视频数据。在明尼阿波利斯选择多个交叉口（含信号和无信号），在干预措施安装前和安装后第1周、第2周进行重复监测，对比分析平均车速、85%分位车速及通过交通量的变化。

Result: 无信号交叉口：平均车速下降最高18.75%，85%分位车速下降最高16.56%，通过交通量减少最高12.2%。信号交叉口：除一个地点外，平均车速下降最高20.0%，85%分位车速下降最高17.19%。所有结果均表明软干预措施显著降低了车速，具有交通稳静化效果。

Conclusion: 临时行人避难岛和路缘扩展等软性基础设施能有效降低车辆速度、减少通过交通量，提升道路安全。基于AI的方法（深度学习+透视速度估计）能够利用现有CCTV实现快速、低成本的交通政策效果评估，为数据驱动的交通管理提供了可行工具。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) and computer vision are transforming transportation data collection. This study introduces an AI-enabled analytics framework leveraging existing CCTV infrastructure to evaluate the impact of soft interventions, such as temporary pedestrian refuges and curb extensions, on vehicle speed and safety. Using deep learning and perspective-based speed estimation, we evaluated driver behavior before and after interventions, with repeated post-installation monitoring in Week 1 and Week 2, in Minneapolis. Findings reveal that at unsignalized intersections, mean and 85th-percentile speeds fell by up to 18.75% and 16.56%, respectively, while pass-through traffic decreased by as much as 12.2%. Signalized intersections showed comparable reductions except one location, with mean and 85th-percentile speeds dropping by up to 20.0% and 17.19%. These results demonstrate the traffic-calming effectiveness of soft infrastructure and underscore the utility of AI-powered methods for rapid, low-cost, and evidence-based transport policy evaluation.

</details>


### [163] [When Helpfulness Becomes Sycophancy: Sycophancy is a Boundary Failure Between Social Alignment and Epistemic Integrity in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05403)
*Jiechen Li,Catherine A. Barry,Rishika Randev,Janet Chen,Ella Jorgensen,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证LLM中的谄媚是社会对齐与 epistemic integrity 之间的边界失效问题，提出了一个三条件框架来界定谄媚，并给出了包含对齐目标、机制和严重程度的分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将谄媚操作为赞同不正确用户信念、立场反转等外部行为，只捕捉了显性形式，而遗漏了涉及 epistemic integrity 和社会对齐的微妙边界失效。

Method: 提出三条件框架：(1) 用户表达信念/偏好/自我概念的线索；(2) 模型通过对齐行为向该线索偏移；(3) 这种偏移损害了 epistemic accuracy、独立推理或适当纠正。同时提出包含对齐目标、机制和严重程度的分类体系。

Result: 提出了一个更精确的谄媚界定框架和分类体系，区分了单纯 agree 与真正的谄媚行为。

Conclusion: 呼吁边界感知评估、结构化评分标准和缓解策略，并讨论了与其他谄媚观点的关系。

Abstract: This position paper argues that sycophancy in LLMs is a boundary failure between social alignment and epistemic integrity. Existing work often operationalizes sycophancy through external behavior such as agreement with incorrect user beliefs, position reversals, or deviation from an objective standard of correctness. These formulations capture only overt forms of the phenomenon and leave subtler boundary failures involving epistemic integrity and social alignment underspecified. We argue that sycophancy should not be understood as agreement alone, but as alignment behavior that displaces independent epistemic judgment. To clarify this boundary, we propose a three-condition framework for sycophancy. First, the user expresses a cue in the form of a belief, preference, or self-concept. Second, the model shifts toward that cue through alignment behavior. Third, this shift compromises epistemic accuracy, independent reasoning, or appropriate correction. We also introduce a taxonomy for classifying sycophancy, consisting of alignment targets, mechanisms, and severity. The paper concludes by discussing implications for alignment evaluation and argues for boundary-aware assessment, structured rubrics, and mitigation strategies, while situating these proposals alongside alternative views of sycophancy.

</details>


### [164] [LaTA: A Drop-in, FERPA-Compliant Local-LLM Autograder for Upper-Division STEM Coursework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05410)
*Jesse A. Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LaTA（LaTeX教学助手），一个完全运行在本地硬件上的开源自动批改系统，专为已采用LaTeX工作流的工程和物理课程设计。该系统在俄勒冈州立大学ME 373课程中部署，为约200名学生每周批改作业，边际成本为零，错误率仅0.02-0.04%，且使用该系统的学生在期中考试中表现提升约11%，期末考试提升约8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM批改系统大多依赖第三方API，这违反了FERPA（家庭教育权利和隐私法案）并对机构造成数据风险，同时需要大量修改现有作业设计。为了在不牺牲隐私和安全的前提下实现自动化批改，作者提出了一个完全本地化、无需修改现有教学流程的解决方案。

Method: LaTA采用四阶段流水线：1）摄取（ingest）：接收学生LaTeX提交；2）分段（segment）：将作业拆分为可单独评估的片段；3）批改（grade）：使用本地托管的开源思维链LLM（gpt-oss:120b），将学生作业与教师提供的参考答案进行对比，并应用YAML格式的二元逐项评分规则；4）报告（report）：生成批改报告。整个系统运行在一台Mac Studio上，无需联网。

Result: 在2026年冬季学期ME 373课程中，LaTA成功批改了约200名学生的每周作业，每份作业耗时1-3分钟，边际成本为零。经教师确认的批改错误率约为每条规则项的0.02-0.04%。与教师以往传统批改的班级相比，LaTA批改的班级期中考试成绩高出约11%，期末考试成绩高出约8%。学生自我评估的信心在所有学习目标上均有显著提升（N=159，Δ≥+1.49 Likert点，p<10^{-27}）。

Conclusion: LaTA证明了在完全本地化的环境中使用开源LLM进行高精度、低成本的大规模作业批改是可行的，不仅能保护学生数据隐私，还能显著提升学生学习效果。该系统代码以AGPLv3许可证开源发布。

Abstract: Large-language-model (LLM) graders promise to relieve the grading burden of upper-division STEM courses, but most deployments to date send student work to third-party APIs, violating FERPA and exposing institutions to data risk while requiring substantial assignment modification. We present $\textbf{LaTA}\ (\textit{LaTeX Teaching Assistant})$, a drop-in, open-source autograder that runs entirely on commodity on-premises hardware and assumes a LaTeX-native workflow already adopted by many engineering and physics courses. LaTA implements a four-stage pipeline (ingest, segment, grade, report) using a locally hosted open-weight chain-of-thought LLM grader (gpt-oss:120b) that compares student work to an instructor-authored reference solution and applies a YAML rubric with binary per-item scoring. We deployed LaTA in Winter~2026 in ME 373 (Mechanical Engineering Methods) at Oregon State University, grading every weekly assignment for approximately 200 students on a single Mac Studio at \$0 marginal cost per assignment and 1--3 minutes of wall-clock time per submission, enabling regrading of corrected assignments and greatly expanded TA office hour offerings. The instructor-confirmed grading-error rate held at roughly $0.02$--$0.04\%$ per rubric line item across the term. Relative to the same instructor's previous traditionally-graded cohort, the LaTA-graded cohort outperformed by approximately $11\%$ on the midterm exam and $8\%$ on the final exam, and reported large gains in self-assessed confidence on every stated learning objective ($N = 159$ survey responses, $Δ\geq +1.49$ Likert points, $p < 10^{-27}$ on every comparison). We release the code under AGPLv3.

</details>


### [165] [From History to State: Constant-Context Skill Learning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05413)
*Haoyang Xie,Xinyuan Wang,Yancheng Wang,Puda Zhao,Feng Ju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种"恒定上下文技能学习"框架，将可复用的智能体工作流从提示词转移到模型权重中，通过轻量级任务族模块和确定性状态跟踪器，在保持性能的同时将每轮提示词令牌减少2-7倍，并在ALFWorld、WebShop和SciWorld上取得了优异结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体面临隐私-成本-能力的矛盾：云端模型效果好但会暴露敏感中间上下文，本地模型保护隐私但可靠性不足；且两者都因长技能提示和历史记录而反复付出高额成本。

Method: 提出constant-context skill learning框架，将可复用工作流学习到轻量级任务族模块的权重中；通过确定性跟踪器从任务进度生成状态块并提供子目标奖励，每个模块通过步骤级SFT训练和在线RL优化；推理时仅依赖当前观测和紧凑状态块。

Result: Qwen3-8B的SFT+RL在ALFWorld上达到89.6%未见任务成功率，WebShop上76.8%，SciWorld上66.4%；每轮提示词令牌比基线减少2-7倍。

Conclusion: 程序性上下文可以从提示词转移到模型权重中，在匹配或超越强基线性能的同时大幅降低提示词开销，为隐私-成本-能力困境提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to operate browsers, files, code and tools, making personal assistants a natural deployment target. Yet personal agents face a privacy-cost-capability tension: cloud models execute multi-step workflows well but expose sensitive intermediate context to external APIs, while local models preserve privacy but remain less reliable. Both settings also pay repeatedly for long skill prompts and growing histories. We propose constant-context skill learning, a context-to-weights framework for recurring agent workflows: reusable procedures are learned in lightweight task-family modules, while inference conditions only on the current observation and a compact state block. A deterministic tracker renders this state block from task progress and supplies aligned subgoal rewards, so each module can be trained with step-level SFT and refined through online RL. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld, our agents achieve strong performance across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.1-8B. With Qwen3-8B, SFT+RL reaches 89.6\% unseen success on ALFWorld, 76.8\% success on WebShop, and 66.4\% unseen success on SciWorld. They match or exceed strong published agent-training results while reducing prompt tokens per turn by 2--7$\times$ relative to controlled ReAct prompting baselines, showing that procedural context can be moved from prompts into weights.

</details>


### [166] [The Geopolitics of AI Safety: A Causal Analysis of Regional LLM Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05427)
*Alif Al Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于概率图模型（PGM）的因果框架，通过do算子干预提示中的人口统计特征，对7个不同地区（美国、欧洲、阿联酋、中国、印度）的指令微调大语言模型进行安全机制审计。结果表明：标准公平性指标因忽视语境毒性而高估人口统计偏见；西方模型对特定群体有更高的因果拒绝率，而东方模型总体干预率低但对区域人口具有定向敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性评估主要依赖观察性偏差测量，该方法无法区分话题本身的固有毒性与被测试人口统计特征之间的混杂效应，导致对模型安全机制公平性的评估存在偏误。

Method: 引入概率图模型（PGM）框架，应用Pearl的do算子从因果上隔离向提示中注入人口统计特征的因果效应。在7个指令微调模型（Llama-3.1-8B、Gemma-2-9B、Mistral-7B-v0.3、Falcon3-7B、Qwen2.5-7B、DeepSeek-7B、Airavata-7B）上，使用ToxiGen和BOLD两个数据集进行大规模实证分析。

Result: 观察性偏差与干预性偏差存在差异，标准公平性指标因未控制语境毒性而高估人口统计偏差。西方模型对特定人口群体有更高的因果拒绝率，东方模型总体干预率较低但对区域人口具有定向敏感性。

Conclusion: 人口统计敏感性的过度触发会限制下游应用中的良性话语，需在安全机制设计中考虑因果公平性。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are integrated into global software systems, ensuring equitable safety guardrails is a critical requirement. Current fairness evaluations predominantly measure bias observationally, a methodology confounded by the inherent toxicity of topics naturally paired with specific demographics in testing datasets. This study introduces a Probabilistic Graphical Model (PGM) framework to audit LLM safety mechanisms causally. By applying Pearl's do-operator, we mathematically isolate the causal effect of injecting a cultural demographic into a prompt. We conduct a large-scale empirical analysis across seven instruction-tuned models spanning diverse origins: the United States (Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B), Europe (Mistral-7B-v0.3), the UAE (Falcon3-7B), China (Qwen2.5-7B, DeepSeek-7B), and India (Airavata-7B). Utilizing two distinct datasets (ToxiGen and BOLD), the findings reveal a disparity between observational and interventional bias, demonstrating that standard fairness metrics can overestimate demographic bias by failing to account for context toxicity. Furthermore, the causal probabilities indicate distinct alignment trends: Western models exhibit higher causal refusal rates for specific demographic groups, whereas Eastern models demonstrate low overall intervention rates with targeted sensitivities toward regional demographics. We discuss the implications of these biases, highlighting how demographic-sensitive over-triggering restricts benign discourse in downstream applications.

</details>


### [167] [Authorization Propagation in Multi-Agent AI Systems: Identity Governance as Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05440)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出多智能体AI系统存在一个独特的授权传播问题（非提示注入），将其形式化为工作流级属性，识别了三个子问题（传递性委托、聚合推理、时间有效性），并推导出七个结构需求。核心主张是身份治理必须作为基础设施，在编排逻辑扩展前进行设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于智能体AI安全讨论过度聚焦于提示注入，而多智能体系统中非人类主体跨边界检索数据、委托任务和合成结果时产生的授权问题未被充分认识，现有RBAC/ABAC/ReBAC模型无法完全解决。

Method: 形式化定义授权传播为工作流级属性，识别三个子问题，推导七个结构需求，并结合现有工作（调用绑定能力令牌、任务范围授权信封、依赖图策略执行、执行计数撤销）进行收敛性分析，最后通过生产级企业AI平台提供初步实证。

Result: 识别了传递性委托、聚合推理和时间有效性三个子问题；推导出七个结构需求；实证表明常规系统行为（而非仅对抗行为）已产生模型预测的失败。

Conclusion: 身份治理必须作为基础设施，持续评估、在每个交互边界强制执行，并在编排逻辑扩展前设计到系统中。

Abstract: The security discussion around agentic AI focuses heavily on prompt injection. This paper argues that multi-agent systems also create a distinct authorization problem: maintaining authorization invariants as non-human principals retrieve data, delegate tasks, and synthesize results across changing boundaries. We call this problem authorization propagation. It is not reducible to prompt injection and is not fully addressed by classical access-control models such as RBAC, ABAC, or ReBAC. The paper formalizes authorization propagation as a workflow-level property, identifies three sub-problems (transitive delegation, aggregation inference, and temporal validity), and derives seven structural requirements for authorization architectures in multi-agent AI systems. Recent work on invocation-bound capability tokens, task-scoped authorization envelopes, dependency-graph policy enforcement, and execution-count revocation demonstrates that the field is converging on the problem, but not yet on a complete architecture. The central claim is that identity governance must be treated as infrastructure: evaluated continuously, enforced at every interaction boundary, and designed into the system before orchestration logic is allowed to scale. Preliminary implementation evidence from a production enterprise AI platform shows that ordinary system behavior, not only adversarial action, already produces the failures this model predicts.

</details>


### [168] [Agentic Discovery of Exchange-Correlation Density Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05460)
*Titouan Duston,Jiashu Liang,Yuanheng Wang,Weihao Gao,Xuelan Wen,Nan Sheng,Weiluo Ren,Yang Sun,Yixiao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型的智能体搜索系统（SAFS），通过迭代的"计划-执行-总结"循环自动设计新的交换关联泛函，发现的SAFS26-a泛函在热化学数据集上比ωB97M-V基线提升约9%，同时警示了AI可能利用非物理捷径欺骗基准测试的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交换关联泛函的开发依赖人工设计，结合物理直觉、精确约束和经验拟合，效率有限。本文旨在利用大语言模型的能力，实现系统化、自动化的泛函设计流程，替代人工驱动的研究范式。

Method: 系统采用智能体搜索框架：由LLM基于进化历史提出结构化的泛函形式变化，通过迭代的"计划-执行-总结"循环运行。改进通过优化泛函参数在标准热化学数据集上的表现来量化，并在保留子集上评估性能。

Result: 发现的SAFS26-a泛函在热化学数据集上比黄金标准ωB97M-V基线提升了约9%。

Conclusion: AI辅助科学中，能发现真正改进的模型同样有能力利用非物理捷径来欺骗基准测试；将领域知识转化为明确强制约束仍然是保证结果科学可靠的必要条件。

Abstract: The development of accurate exchange-correlation (XC) functionals remains a longstanding challenge in density functional theory (DFT). The vast majority of XC functionals have been hand designed by human researchers combining physical insight, exact constraints, and empirical fitting. Recent advances in large language models enable a systematic, automated alternative to this human-driven design loop. This report presents an agentic search system in which an LLM proposes structured functional-form changes guided by evolutionary history. The system attempts to improve functional performance through an iterative plan-execute-summarize loop, where improvements are measurable by optimizing functional parameters against a standard thermochemistry dataset, then evaluating performance on a held-out subset. The strongest discovered functional, SAFS26-a (Seed Agentic Functional Search 2026), improves upon the gold-standard ωB97M-V baseline by ~9%. These results also surface a cautionary lesson for AI-assisted science: models powerful enough to discover genuine improvements are equally capable of exploiting unphysical shortcuts to game the benchmark; domain expertise translated into explicitly enforced constraints remains essential to keeping results scientifically grounded.

</details>


### [169] [LANTERN: LLM-Augmented Neurosymbolic Transfer with Experience-Gated Reasoning Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05478)
*Mahyar Alinejad,Yue Wang,Amrit Singh Bedi,George Atia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LANTERN是一个统一的多源神经符号迁移学习框架，通过LLM生成任务自动机、语义嵌入聚合多源策略、自适应教师-学生门控机制，在资源管理、导航和控制领域实现40-60%的样本效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号迁移方法依赖手动指定的任务自动机、仅使用单一源任务、采用固定的知识集成机制无法适应源任务相关性变化，亟需一个统一的多源自适应框架来提升迁移学习的可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出LANTERN框架，包含三个核心组件：(i) 利用大语言模型从自然语言任务描述生成确定性有限自动机；(ii) 基于语义嵌入的多源策略聚合，通过跨任务相似度加权；(iii) 基于时序差分误差和语义不确定性的自适应教师-学生门控机制。

Result: 在资源管理、导航和控制等多个领域，LANTERN相比现有基线方法实现了40-60%的样本效率提升，并且在源任务与目标任务对齐不佳的情况下仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 多源自适应加权的神经符号迁移方法能够显著提升符号RL设置下的可扩展性和鲁棒性，验证了自适应知识集成机制在复杂迁移场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Transfer learning in reinforcement learning (RL) seeks to accelerate learning in new tasks by leveraging knowledge from related sources. Existing neurosymbolic transfer methods, however, typically rely on manually specified task automata, assume a single source task, and use fixed knowledge-integration mechanisms that cannot adapt to varying source relevance. We propose LANTERN, a unified framework for multi-source neurosymbolic transfer that addresses these limitations through three components: (i) deterministic finite automata generated from natural language task descriptions using large language models, (ii) semantic embedding-based aggregation of multiple source policies weighted by cross-task similarity, and (iii) adaptive teacher-student gating based on temporal-difference error and semantic uncertainty. Across domains spanning resource management, navigation, and control, LANTERN achieves 40-60% improvements in sample efficiency over existing baselines while remaining robust to poorly aligned sources. These results demonstrate that multi-source, adaptively weighted neurosymbolic transfer can improve scalability and robustness in symbolic RL settings.

</details>


### [170] [FinRAG-12B: A Production-Validated Recipe for Grounded Question Answering in Banking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05482)
*Denys Katerenchuk,Pablo Duboue,Keelan Evanini,David Gondek,Nithin Govindugari,Olivier Allauzen,Joshua Baptiste,David J More,Joshua Schechter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一且数据高效的框架，用于训练面向银行业的领域专用大语言模型，在仅使用1.43亿 token 的情况下，通过数据生成管道、校准拒答机制和端到端部署方法，实现了优于GPT-4.1的引用准确性、可控的拒答率（12%），并在40多家金融机构部署后查询解决率提升7.1个百分点，响应速度快3-5倍，成本降低20-50倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在各行业快速普及，但在银行业面临高精度、合规监管以及可验证、有据可依的响应等严格要求，现有模型难以同时满足这些约束。

Method: 1) 数据生成管道：结合LLM-as-a-Judge过滤、引用注释和课程学习，仅用1.43亿 token；2) 校准拒答机制：在22%的不可回答样本上训练，使拒答率达到12%；3) 端到端方法：从数据整理到量化服务部署的全流程。

Result: 12B模型在答案质量上优于GPT-4.1，引用准确性显著提高；拒答率12%优于基础模型（4.3%）且避免了GPT-4.1的过度拒答（20.2%）；在40多家金融机构部署后查询解决率提升7.1个百分点（p<0.001）；响应速度3-5倍，成本降低20-50倍。

Conclusion: 该框架在保证高准确率和合规性的同时，实现了高效、低成本的银行业LLM部署，验证了数据高效训练和校准拒答机制在实际金融场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly being adopted across various domains. However, their adoption in banking industry faces resistance due to demands for high accuracy, regulatory compliance, and the need for verifiable and grounded responses. We present a unified, data-efficient framework for training grounded domain-specific LLMs that optimizes answer quality, citation grounding, and calibrated refusal under real-world deployment constraints. First, we describe a data generation pipeline that combines LLM-as-a-Judge filtering, citation annotation, and curriculum learning with only 143M tokens. The resulting 12B model achieves high answer quality outperforming GPT-4.1 on citation grounding, with a modest citation tradeoff versus the untuned base. Second, we propose a calibrated refusal mechanism: training on 22% unanswerable examples yield a 12% "I don't know" rate, substantially improving over the base model's unsafe 4.3% rate while avoiding GPT-4.1's over-refusal (20.2%). Third, we present an end-to-end methodology spanning from data curation to quantized serving. The system is deployed at 40+ financial institutions, achieving a 7.1 percentage point improvement in query resolution (p < 0.001). Additionally, the model delivers 3-5x faster responses at 20-50x lower cost compared to GPT-4.1.

</details>


### [171] [FoodCHA: Multi-Modal LLM Agent for Fine-Grained Food Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05499)
*Woojin Lee,Pranav Mekkoth,Ye Tian,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FoodCHA 是一个多模态智能体框架，通过分层决策和渐进式预测机制，利用轻量级 Moondream-2B 视觉语言模型，在食品识别任务中显著提升了类别、子类别及烹饪风格的识别精度，在 FoodNExTDB 数据集上分别比 Food-Llama-3.2-11B 提升了 13.8%、38.2% 和 153.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在细粒度食品属性（如烹饪风格）识别上表现不佳，而开放生成的视觉语言模型会产生非规范标签，限制了实际部署。作者提出 FoodCHA 以解决食品识别中类内相似性高、多物品共存及细粒度属性判别困难的问题。

Method: 将食品识别重构为分层决策过程：利用高层类别引导子类别识别，再以子类别引导烹饪风格识别，实现渐进式锚定预测。采用轻量级 Moondream-2B 视觉语言模型提供推理能力，同时降低计算和内存开销。

Result: 在 FoodNExTDB 数据集上，FoodCHA 在类别、子类别和烹饪风格识别精度上分别比 Food-Llama-3.2-11B 提升 13.8%、38.2% 和 153.2%。

Conclusion: FoodCHA 通过分层决策框架有效提升了食品识别中细粒度属性的判别能力，同时轻量模型设计使其适合实际部署，为饮食监测应用提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of camera-equipped mobile devices and wearables has enabled convenient capture of meal images, making food recognition a key component for real time dietary monitoring. However, real-world food images present challenges due to high intra-class similarity and the frequent presence of multiple food items within a single image. While deep learning models achieve strong performance in coarse grained classification, they often struggle to capture fine-grained attributes such as cooking style. Moreover, open-ended generation in modern vision-language models can produce non-canonical labels, limiting their practical deployment. We propose FoodCHA, a multimodal agentic framework that reformulates food recognition as a hierarchical decision-making process. By progressively anchoring predictions, FoodCHA guides subcategory identification using high-level categories and guides cooking style recognition using subcategories, improving semantic consistency and attribute-level discrimination. To ensure practical deployability, FoodCHA utilizes the compact Moondream-2B vision language model, which provides strong reasoning capability while maintaining lower computational and memory overhead. Experiments on FoodNExTDB show that FoodCHA outperforms Food-Llama-3.2-11B by 13.8% and 38.2% in category and subcategory recognition precision, respectively, and achieves a striking 153.2% improvement in cooking style classification precision.

</details>


### [172] [Housing Potential Common Data Model and City Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05535)
*Megan Katsumi,Mark Fox,Anderson Wong,Divnoor Chatha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出住房潜力通用数据模型（HPCDM），旨在打破数据孤岛、实现多源数据集成与互操作，并构建城市数字孪生与仪表盘应用以验证其实践可行性，同时识别关键采纳障碍并提出缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有住房潜力评估涉及分区、土地利用、人口特征和公共服务等多维度数据，但数据分散在各自孤岛中，缺乏统一标准来支持跨数据集的集成与互操作。

Method: 提出住房潜力通用数据模型（HPCDM）作为标准框架；基于该模型构建城市数字孪生系统并开发试点仪表盘应用；通过实证评估验证模型有效性。

Result: 成功评估了HPCDM模型，并展示了其在城市数字孪生和仪表盘应用中的实际部署效果；同时识别出关键采纳障碍。

Conclusion: HPCDM能够有效支持住房潜力分析所需的多源数据集成与互操作，但需克服已识别的采纳障碍才能实现广泛推广。

Abstract: The evaluation of housing potential requires consideration of a location from multiple perspectives, ranging from zoning and land use to population characteristics and access to services. This research introduces the Housing Potential Common Data Model (HPCDM) to overcome existing data silos, serving as a standard to support integration and interoperability across the diverse range of datasets that are required for housing potential analysis. This report details the evaluation of the model along with the creation of a City Digital Twin for housing and a pilot dashboard application to demonstrate a practical implementation. Beyond the technical framework, this work identifies critical barriers to adoption and provides actionable mitigation strategies for urban planners and stakeholders.

</details>


### [173] [AgenticRAG: Agentic Retrieval for Enterprise Knowledge Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05538)
*Susheel Suresh,Hazel Mak,Shangpo Chou,Fred Kroon,Sahil Bhatnagar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgenticRAG 提出了一种轻量级智能体框架，通过让推理LLM自主使用搜索、查找、打开和摘要等工具，替代传统RAG中单次检索的固定候选集限制。在BRIGHT、WixQA、FinanceBench三个基准上取得显著提升，其中从单次检索到智能体工具使用的转变带来了5.9倍的改进，证明了该方法在企业生产环境中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RAG流水线将过多证据负担压在搜索栈上，把语言模型限制在检索过程深早期确定的固定候选集中，导致模型缺乏自主探索和分析证据的能力。

Method: 在现有企业搜索基础设施之上叠加一个轻量级智能体框架，为推理LLM配备搜索、查找、打开和摘要四种工具，使其能够迭代检索信息、在文档内导航并自主分析证据。

Result: BRIGHT上recall@1达49.6%（比最佳嵌入基线高21.8个百分点），WixQA上事实性达0.96（相对提升13%），FinanceBench上答案正确率92%（与真实验证集只有2个百分点差距）。消融实验表明智能体工具使用是最大贡献因素（5.9倍提升）。

Conclusion: AgenticRAG通过将检索过程从单次固定候选集转变为模型驱动的迭代探索，显著提升了RAG在事实性和答案正确性上的表现，适合企业级生产环境部署。

Abstract: We present AgenticRAG, a practical agentic harness for retrieval and analysis over enterprise knowledge bases. Standard RAG pipelines place significant burden of grounding on the search stack, constraining the language model to a fixed candidate set chosen deep in the retrieval process. Our approach reduces this overdependence by layering a lightweight harness on top of existing enterprise search infrastructure, equipping a reasoning LLM with search, find, open, and summarize tools enabling the model to iteratively retrieve information, navigate within documents, and analyze evidence autonomously. On three open benchmarks we observe substantial gains: $49.6\%$ recall@1 on BRIGHT (+21.8 pp over the best embedding baseline), 0.96 factuality on WixQA ($+13\%$ relative improvement), and $92\%$ answer correctness on FinanceBench--within 2 pp of oracle access to true evidence. Ablation studies show that the most significant factor is the shift from single-shot retrieval to agentic tool use ($5.9\times$ improvement), while multi-query search and in-document navigation contribute to both quality and efficiency. We present various design choices in our agentic harness that were informed by pre-production deployments. Our results demonstrate its suitability for real-world enterprise production environments.

</details>


### [174] [SPARK: Self-Play with Asymmetric Reward from Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05546)
*Hyobin Park,Taeseop Kim,Dong-Geol Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPARK是一个将知识图谱作为自博弈结构基础的科学文献关系推理框架，通过构建统一的多文档知识图谱来生成关系推理问题并提供可验证的奖励信号，实验表明其在多跳推理任务上优于基于无结构语料的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自博弈强化学习在数学和编程等具有形式化可验证结构的领域表现出色，但将其扩展到科学文献领域面临挑战：文献中多模态元素之间的关系很少在文本中明确表达，这使得关系推理问题的自动生成变得困难，且奖励信号的可靠性降低。

Method: 提出SPARK框架，自动从多文档科学文献构建统一知识图谱(KG)；利用KG中多模态节点间的路径生成关系推理问题；基于KG中的结构化事实提供可验证的奖励计算；采用单一小视觉语言模型(sVLM)在Proposer和Solver角色间交替，在信息不对称条件下对抗固定KG进行自博弈。

Result: 在公开基准和自构建的跨文档多跳QA数据集上评估，SPARK持续优于基于无结构语料的自博弈基线，且随着跳数增加性能差距扩大。

Conclusion: KG结构基础对关系性多跳推理的贡献超越了无结构语料基础所能提供的，该方法可自然扩展到在线适应场景。

Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning has shown strong performance in domains with formally verifiable structure, such as mathematics and coding, where both problem generation and reward computation can be grounded in explicit rules. Extending this paradigm to scientific literature is more challenging: the relationships among multi-modal elements within and across documents are rarely made explicit in text, which makes automatic generation of relational reasoning questions difficult and weakens the reliability of reward signals. We propose SPARK (Self-Play with Asymmetric Reward from Knowledge Graphs), a framework that automatically constructs a unified knowledge graph (KG) from multi-document scientific literature and uses it as the structural basis for self-play. KG paths over multimodal nodes serve as a source for generating relational reasoning questions, and structured facts stored in the KG provide a basis for verifiable reward computation. A single small vision-language model (sVLM) alternates between Proposer and Solver roles under information asymmetry against a fixed KG, a design that we believe can be naturally extended toward online adaptation in future work. We evaluate SPARK on public benchmarks and a self-constructed cross-document multi-hop QA dataset. Results show that SPARK consistently outperforms flat-corpus-based self-play baselines, and the performance gap widens as hop count increases, suggesting that KG-structure grounding contributes to relational multi-hop reasoning beyond what unstructured corpus grounding can provide.

</details>


### [175] [Who Prices Cognitive Labor in the Age of Agents? A Position on Compute-Anchored Wages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05558)
*Siqi Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证AI智能体不是无限弹性的劳动力，而是将算力资本转化为认知劳动的生产技术。由此导出“算力锚定工资”上限：人类认知工资受限于算力资本的租金率、算力密集度和相对生产率之积。劳动力市场不再是认知劳动的定价者，算力资本市场才是。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正一种广泛存在的经济直觉误导——即认为AI智能体因零边际复制成本而构成无限弹性的劳动力供给，从而将认知劳动工资推至零。作者指出这种直觉在机制上是错误的，但结论部分正确，且这一纠正在理论和政策层面都具有重要意义。

Method: 基于教科书级要素定价框架（Mankiw 2020），将AI智能体建模为将算力资本（K_c）转化为有效认知劳动单位（L_A）的生产技术。通过推导“算力锚定工资”（CAW）上界，利用CES聚合模型区分可替代与互补性任务，分析要素份额变化，得出均衡工资锚定于算力资本市场而非劳动力市场的结论。

Result: 推导出竞争性人类工资的上界为 λ·k·r_c（r_c为算力资本租金率，k为单单位有效智能体劳动的算力密集度，λ为人类与智能体的相对生产率）。在可替代任务上，人类的认知工资被算力资本市场的均衡条件所锚定，而非由劳动力市场供求决定。同时发现技能偏向型技术变化的方向在互补性任务上会发生反转。

Conclusion: 认知劳动的定价者已从劳动力市场转移到算力资本市场。这一重新框架对于理解AI时代收入分配、要素份额变化及相关政策设计具有关键的理论修正意义。

Abstract: A natural intuition about the economics of AI agents is that, because agents can be replicated at near-zero marginal cost, they constitute a labor input in infinitely elastic supply, and therefore drive cognitive-labor wages to zero. We argue this framing is wrong in mechanism but partially correct in conclusion, and that the correction matters for both theory and policy. \textbf{Agents are not labor; they are a production technology that converts compute capital $K_c$ into effective units of cognitive labor $L_A$.} Once this is recognized, the elastic-supply margin that anchors the equilibrium wage migrates from the labor market to the compute capital market. Building on the textbook factor-pricing framework \citep{mankiw2020}, we derive a \emph{Compute-Anchored Wage} (CAW) bound stating that, on tasks where human and agent cognitive labor are substitutes, the competitive human wage is bounded above by $λ\cdot k \cdot r_c$, where $r_c$ is the rental rate of compute capital, $k$ is the compute intensity of one effective agent-labor unit, and $λ$ is the relative human-to-agent productivity. We generalize the result through CES aggregation, separate substitutable from complementary tasks (yielding a directional inversion of skill-biased technical change), and discuss factor-share consequences. The position is concise: \emph{the price-setter for cognitive labor is no longer the labor market.}

</details>


### [176] [Nonsense Helps: Prompt Space Perturbation Broadens Reasoning Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05566)
*Langlin Huang,Chengsong Huang,Jinyuan Li,Donghong Cai,Yuyi Yang,Jiaxin Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LoPE方法，通过在提示前添加随机拉丁占位文本扰动，解决GRPO在复杂推理任务中的"零优势问题"，有效打破探索瓶颈，显著提升模型在困难问题上的训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在复杂任务中常出现"零优势问题"：当查询的所有采样轨迹均失败时，相对优势归零，导致模型无法获得有效训练信号，浪费数据和计算资源。简单增加采样预算无法根本解决，因为静态采样策略限制了推理探索。

Method: 提出LoPE（Lorem Perturbation for Exploration）框架：在重新采样前，向提示中随机拼接由Lorem Ipsum词汇（伪拉丁占位文本）组成的序列，通过对提示空间施加与任务无关的扰动，使模型输出分布发生偏移，解锁针对难题的正交推理路径。

Result: 在1.7B、4B和7B模型上的实验表明，LoPE显著优于使用原始提示进行重新采样。进一步分析发现，其他低困惑度的基于拉丁语的随机序列同样可作为有效的扰动。

Conclusion: LoPE被确立为一种在LLM强化学习中拓宽探索空间的强基线方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, particularly Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has significantly advanced the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, in complex tasks, GRPO frequently suffers from the ``zero-advantage problem'': when all sampled rollouts for a query fail, the relative advantage collapses to zero. Consequently, the model loses effective training signals for these questions, wasting the training data and computational budget. While simply increasing the sampling budget for these questions is a common remedy, the static sampling policy inherently constrains reasoning exploration, limiting the success rate. In this paper, we propose Lorem Perturbation for Exploration (LoPE), a simple yet effective training framework to break this exploration bottleneck. We posit that task-irrelevant prompt-space perturbations can shift the model's output distribution enough to unlock orthogonal reasoning pathways for hard questions. Specifically, LoPE prepends sequences stochastically assembled from Lorem Ipsum vocabulary (a pseudo-Latin placeholder text) to the prompts before resampling. Experiments across 1.7B, 4B, and 7B models demonstrate that LoPE significantly outperforms resampling with the original prompts. Further analysis reveals that other Latin-based random sequences with low perplexity are also effective perturbations. Our results establish LoPE as a strong baseline for broadening exploration in LLM reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [177] [Locality-aware Private Class Identification for Domain Adaptation with Extreme Label Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05567)
*Chuan-Xian Ren,Cheng-Jun Guo,Hong Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对域适应中标签空间包含关系（存在私有类）的问题，基于最优传输的局部输运和度量特性，提出了一种 locality-aware 私有类识别方法（通过传输质量的得分函数），并构建了可靠的基于最优传输的域适应方法（ReOT），在理论上证明了识别有效性，提供了极端标签偏移下的泛化上界，实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有私有类识别方法假设私有类的偏移足够大以被视为异常值，但实际中同一共享类内部的方差可能远大于私有类与共享类之间的差异，这一假设不成立。因此需要更鲁棒的方法来识别私有类并减轻其负面影响。

Method: 基于最优传输的局部输运和度量特性，提出一种 locality-aware 的私有类识别方法，以传输质量的得分函数形式实现。进一步提出 ReOT 方法，在最小化分类风险的同时，学习识别出的共享类与私有类之间的分离聚类结构，避免共享-私有样本对的错误匹配，实现类内可靠传输以缓解类条件分布差异。

Result: 在理论上证明了所提得分函数对共享类和私有类样本的强区分能力，并提供了极端标签偏移场景下目标风险的泛化上界，表明 ReOT 可最小化该上界。大量基准实验验证了 ReOT 的有效性。

Conclusion: ReOT 通过 locality-aware 的私有类识别和可靠的最优传输机制，有效解决了标签空间包含关系下的域适应问题，为处理私有类带来的跨域分类困难提供了理论和实践上的有效方案。

Abstract: Domain adaptation aims to transfer knowledge from a labeled source domain to an unlabeled target domain with different distributions. In real-world scenarios, the label spaces of the two domains often have an inclusion relationship, where some classes exist only in one domain but not the other. These non-overlapping classes are referred to as private classes. Identifying private class samples and mitigating their adverse effects is critical in the literature. Existing methods rely on the assumption that shifts in private classes are large enough to be considered outliers. However, the variance within a single shared class can be significantly larger than the difference between a private class and another shared class, challenging this assumption. Consequently, private classes substantially increase the difficulty of cross-domain classification. To address these issues, based on local transportation and metric properties of optimal transport (OT), a locality-aware private class identification approach is proposed in the form of a score function on transport mass. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is theoretically proven, highlighting the score function's strong ability to distinguish between shared and private class samples. Building on this, we introduce a reliable OT-based method (ReOT) for domain adaptation under severe label shift. ReOT minimizes classification risk while learning the separated cluster structure between the identified shared classes and private classes, effectively avoiding mismatch between shared-private sample pairs, thus ensuring that important knowledge is reliably transported intra-class to mitigate class-conditional discrepancy. Furthermore, a generalization upper bound of the target risk is provided for extreme label shift scenarios, which can be minimized by ReOT. Extensive experiments on benchmarks validate the effectiveness of ReOT.

</details>


### [178] [AlphaCrafter: A Full-Stack Multi-Agent Framework for Cross-Sectional Quantitative Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05580)
*Yishuo Yuan,Jiayi Sheng,Sirui Zeng,Jiaqi Wang,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AlphaCrafter是一个全栈多智能体框架，通过三个专门智能体（Miner、Screener、Trader）构建从因子挖掘到风险约束执行的连续自适应流水线，在CSI 300和S&P 500上取得了优于现有方法的风险调整收益和最低跨试验方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化方法将因子发现、制度自适应选择、风险约束执行等组件在静态或孤立假设下优化，缺乏一个完全自动化、理性驱动的统一量化流水线框架。

Method: 提出AlphaCrafter框架，包含三个智能体：Miner通过LLM引导搜索持续扩展因子池，Screener评估市场状况构建制度条件化因子集成，Trader在显式风险约束下将这些集成转化为量化策略，三者形成闭环横截面交易系统。

Result: 在CSI 300和S&P 500上的大量实验表明，AlphaCrafter在风险调整收益上持续优于最先进基线，并展现出最低的跨试验方差。

Conclusion: 集成且自适应的因子到执行设计能够产生稳健的交易性能。

Abstract: Financial markets are inherently non-stationary, driven by complex interactions among macroeconomic regimes, microstructural frictions, and behavioral dynamics. Building quantitative strategies that remain profitable demands the continuous coupling of factor discovery, regime-adaptive selection, and risk-constrained execution. Prevailing approaches, however, optimize these components under static or isolated assumptions. Factor mining frameworks typically treat alpha discovery as a one-time search process, implicitly assuming that factor efficacy persists across market regimes. Execution-oriented systems often adopt role-playing agent architectures that simulate anthropomorphic trading committees, introducing behavioral noise rather than systematic rationality. Consequently, a fully automated, rationality-driven framework unifying a coherent quantitative pipeline remains absent. We introduce AlphaCrafter, a full-stack multi-agent framework that closes this gap through a continuously adaptive factor-to-execution pipeline, designed to track and respond to evolving market conditions without manual intervention. AlphaCrafter operates via three specialized agents: a Miner that continuously expands the factor pool via LLM-guided search, a Screener that assesses prevailing market conditions to construct regime-conditioned factor ensembles, and a Trader that translates these ensembles into quantitative strategies under explicit risk constraints. Together, these three agents form a closed-loop cross-sectional trading system that adapts holistically to evolving market dynamics. Extensive experiments on CSI 300 and S&P 500 demonstrate that AlphaCrafter consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in risk-adjusted returns while exhibiting the lowest cross-trial variance, confirming that integrated and adaptive factor-to-execution design yields robust trading performance.

</details>


### [179] [Belief Memory: Agent Memory Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05583)
*Junfeng Liao,Qizhou Wang,Jianing Zhu,Bo Du,Rui Yan,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BeliefMem方法，将智能体记忆从单一确定性结论范式转变为保留多个候选结论及其概率，通过Noisy-OR规则更新概率，在部分可观测环境中保留了不确定性信息，避免自我强化的错误传播，在LoCoMo和ALFWorld基准测试中取得最佳平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文LLM智能体依赖外部记忆积累知识，但通常将每次观测存储为单一的确定性结论，忽略了观测本身的局部性和潜在歧义性。这种"一个观测一个结论"的范式会引入自我强化的错误——智能体基于存储的结论行动，从不重新审视替代可能性，且随时间不断强化该结论，导致错误累积。

Method: BeliefMem将记忆范式从每个观测只保留单一结论转变为保留多个候选结论及其概率。每个候选结论作为独立的记忆条目存储，并携带一个概率值；当新观测到来时，通过Noisy-OR规则更新各候选结论的概率；检索时，所有候选结论及其概率一并呈现给智能体，保持替代假设的可视性。

Result: 在LoCoMo和ALFWorld基准测试上，即使在数据有限的情况下，BeliefMem取得了最佳平均性能，显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 这种概率性记忆范式在部分可观测环境中产生了显著的性能提升，为智能体记忆研究探索了新的方向。

Abstract: LLM agents that operate over long context depend on external memory to accumulate knowledge over time. However, existing methods typically store each observation as a single deterministic conclusion (e.g., inferring "API~X failed" from temporary errors), even though such observations are inherently partial and potentially ambiguous. By committing to one conclusion and discarding uncertainty, these methods introduce self-reinforcing error: the agent acts on the stored conclusion, never revisits alternatives, and reinforces the conclusion over time. To address this issue, we propose BeliefMem, which shifts the memory paradigm from committing to a single conclusion per observation to retaining multiple candidate conclusions with their probabilities. Concretely, BeliefMem stores the candidate conclusions as separate memory entries, each carrying a probability that is updated via Noisy-OR rules as new observations arrive. At retrieval, all candidates surface together with their probabilities, keeping alternatives visible to the agent. Since each conclusion in memory retains its probability, BeliefMem preserves the uncertainty that the deterministic paradigm discards, enabling the agent to act with high confidence on well-evidenced knowledge while retaining the capacity to update its confidence when new evidence arrives. Empirical evaluations on LoCoMo and ALFWorld benchmarks show that, even with limited data, BeliefMem achieves the best average performance, remarkably outperforming well-known baselines. More broadly, such probabilistic memory produces substantial gains and explores a new direction for agent memory in partially observable environments.

</details>


### [180] [Causal Probing for Internal Visual Representations in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05593)
*Zehao Deng,Tianjie Ju,Zheng Wu,Liangbo He,Jun Lan,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过因果干预框架（激活引导）系统探究了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中视觉概念的编码机制，发现实体概念呈局部记忆化、抽象概念呈全局分布，并揭示了缩放定律的机械驱动力、感知与生成间的补偿机制，以及感知与推理间的脱节现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型虽有显著成功，但其内部视觉概念编码与接地（grounding）机制尚不明确，现有研究缺乏因果层面的系统分析。

Method: 提出基于激活引导（activation steering）的因果框架，通过系统干预四种视觉概念类别，结合正向/反向引导以及视觉推理任务分析，探究内部表征的编码分布与可操纵性。

Result: (1) 实体概念呈局部记忆化，抽象概念呈全局分布；(2) 模型深度增加对编码分布式抽象概念不可或缺，而实体定位对规模不敏感；(3) 反向引导揭示感知与生成间的潜伏激活补偿机制；(4) 视觉推理中存在感知与推理脱节，模型将几何关系视为静态视觉特征而非触发抽象解题过程。

Conclusion: MLLMs中实体与抽象概念的编码机制存在根本性差异，缩放定律的收益主要来源于对抽象概念的编码需求，且当前模型在感知与推理之间仍存在实质性鸿沟。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable success of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) across diverse tasks, the internal mechanisms governing how they encode and ground distinct visual concepts remain poorly understood. To bridge this gap, we propose a causal framework based on activation steering to actively probe and manipulate internal visual representations. Through systematic intervention across four visual concept categories, our results reveal a divergence in concept encoding: entities exhibit distinct localized memorization, whereas abstract concepts are globally distributed across the network. Critically, this divergence uncovers a mechanistic driver of scaling laws: increasing model depth is indispensable for encoding distributed and complex abstract concepts, whereas entity localization remains remarkably invariant to scale. Furthermore, reverse steering uncovers that blocking explicit output triggers a surge in latent activations, exposing a compensatory mechanism between perception and generation. Finally, extending our analysis to visual reasoning, we expose a disconnect between perception and reasoning although MLLMs successfully recognize geometric relations, they treat them merely as static visual features, failing to trigger the procedural execution necessary for abstract problem-solving.

</details>


### [181] [Text-Graph Synergy: A Bidirectional Verification and Completion Framework for RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05643)
*Jiarui Zhong,Hong Cai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TGS-RAG 提出了一种文本-图双向协同增强的统一框架，通过图到文本的全局投票重排序和文本到图的记忆桥接算法，有效解决了多跳推理中非对称推理流导致的信息孤岛问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文本RAG常检索到逻辑无关的伪证据，图RAG因搜索时剪枝可能丢弃有效推理路径，现有混合方法仅简单拼接或单向增强，未能解决非对称推理流导致的"信息孤岛"问题。

Method: 提出TGS-RAG统一框架，包含双向通道：(i) 图到文本通道：采用全局投票策略对图节点进行重排序，筛选并精炼文本证据；(ii) 文本到图通道：利用记忆桥接算法，通过文本线索主动复活搜索历史中被剪枝的有效推理路径，且无需额外数据库开销。

Result: 在多个多跳推理基准上显著超越现有最优基线，在检索精度与计算效率之间实现了更优的平衡。

Conclusion: TGS-RAG通过文本与图结构的双向协同增强，有效解决了信息孤岛问题，为多跳推理提供了一种高效且精确的新范式。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become a core paradigm for enhancing factual grounding and multi-hop reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs). Traditional text-based RAG often retrieves logically irrelevant pseudo-evidence, while graph-based RAG is frequently hindered by search-time pruning, which may discard potentially valid reasoning paths. Existing hybrid approaches primarily adopt simple evidence concatenation or unidirectional enhancement, which fails to address the fundamental "Information Island" problem caused by asymmetric reasoning flows between unstructured text and structured graphs. We propose \textbf{TGS-RAG}, a unified framework for \textbf{T}ext-\textbf{G}raph \textbf{S}ynergistic enhancement. TGS-RAG introduces a bidirectional mechanism: (i) a \textbf{Graph-to-Text} channel that employs a Global Voting strategy from visited graph nodes to re-rank and refine textual evidence, filtering out semantic noise; and (ii) a \textbf{Text-to-Graph} channel that utilizes the \textbf{Memory-based Orphan Entity Bridging} algorithm. This algorithm utilizes textual cues to proactively resurrect valid but previously pruned reasoning paths from the search history without additional database overhead. Experimental results on multiple multi-hop reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that TGS-RAG significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a superior balance between retrieval precision and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [182] [Large Vision-Language Models Get Lost in Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05668)
*Gongli Xi,Ye Tian,Mengyu Yang,Huahui Yi,Liang Lin,Xiaoshuai Hao,Kun Wang,Wendong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于信息论和几何学的统一框架，用于量化大型视觉语言模型中残差更新的几何与熵特性，揭示了注意力模块是保持子空间的重新配置算子，而FFN是扩展子空间的语义创新算子；进一步实验表明，用预定义值（如高斯噪声）替换学习到的注意力权重在多数数据集上表现相当甚至更优，说明当前机制存在严重的注意力冗余与资源错配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统计归因方法缺乏统一理论基础，无法系统揭示LVLM内部模块的独特作用，因此需要建立一个统一的理论框架来理解注意力与FFN在残差更新中的功能分化。

Method: 基于信息论和几何学构建统一框架，量化残差更新的几何（子空间结构）与熵（信息量）特性，并对注意力与FFN模块分别进行理论分析和实验验证。

Result: 注意力是子空间保持算子（侧重重新配置），FFN是子空间扩展算子（驱动语义创新）；用高斯噪声等预定义值替换注意力权重，在多数数据集上表现不逊于甚至优于原始模型。

Conclusion: 当前LVLM中的注意力机制存在严重的资源错配与冗余，模型实际上是"迷失在注意力中"而非高效利用视觉上下文，未来架构优化应重新审视注意力与FFN的角色分工。

Abstract: Despite the rapid evolution of training paradigms, the decoder backbone of large vision--language models (LVLMs) remains fundamentally rooted in the residual-connection Transformer architecture. Therefore, deciphering the distinct roles of internal modules is critical for understanding model mechanics and guiding architectural optimization. While prior statistical approaches have provided valuable attribution-based insights, they often lack a unified theoretical basis.
